import sys, time, collections, numpy as np
import os; sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
from evolution import EvolutionaryAlgorithm
from environment import SimulationEnvironment
def run(mode, scale, pop=24, ticks=500, map_key="WAREHOUSE", seed=3):
    np.random.seed(seed)
    ea = EvolutionaryAlgorithm(pop_size=pop, mode=mode)
    env = SimulationEnvironment(map_key=map_key, mode=mode, seed=seed, world_scale=scale, dirt_spawn_rate=2)
    env.reset(ea.agents)
    t0 = time.time()
    for t in range(ticks):
        env.step(ea.agents)
    dt = time.time() - t0
    bus = env.comm_bus
    intents = collections.Counter(m.intent for m in bus.chat_archive)
    status = collections.Counter(m.status for m in bus.chat_archive)
    roles = collections.Counter(a.role_id for a in ea.agents)
    print(f"{mode:9s} x{scale} {pop} robot {ticks} tick: {dt:.2f}s ({dt/ticks*1000:.1f} ms/tick)  Ă©lĹ‘={sum(a.alive for a in ea.agents)}")
    print("   szerepek:", dict(roles))
    print("   ĂĽzenetek:", dict(intents))
    print("   stĂˇtuszok:", dict(status), "| elnyomott duplikĂˇlt:", bus.suppressed_duplicates, "sĂˇv:", bus.suppressed_rate, "elĂ©rhetetlen:", bus.unreachable_count)
    return ea, env
for mode in ("CLASSIC", "COOP", "TDM", "ANTARCTIC"):
    run(mode, 1)
run("COOP", 3, pop=30)
run("CLASSIC", 5, pop=40, ticks=300)

=== FILE: C:\Users\dave\Desktop\egyetemgeci\5. felev\Projektmunka\project geminivel\3d verzio\archive\3d\tests\test_agents.py ===
import sys, collections, numpy as np
import os; sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))
from evolution import EvolutionaryAlgorithm
from environment import SimulationEnvironment, CommunicationBus
from agent import RobotAgent, AgentMessage, AgentMemory, Genome
import coordination as co

OK = True
def check(name, cond, extra=""):
    global OK
    OK = OK and bool(cond)
    print(("PASS " if cond else "FAIL ") + name + (f"  [{extra}]" if extra and not cond else ""))

def scenario(mode="COOP", pop=6, genes=None, map_key="COMPACT", seed=1, scale=1):
    np.random.seed(seed)
    ea = EvolutionaryAlgorithm(pop_size=pop, mode=mode)
    env = SimulationEnvironment(map_key=map_key, mode=mode, seed=seed, world_scale=scale, dirt_spawn_rate=0)
    env.reset(ea.agents)
    env.dirt_grid[:] = 0
    for i, a in enumerate(ea.agents):
        g = (genes or {}).get(i, [0.4, 0.5, 0.4, 0.8, 0.5, 0.7, 0.4, 0.5])
        a.genome.genes[:] = np.array(g, dtype=np.float32)
        a.pos = np.array([30.0 + 3.0 * i, 8.0], dtype=np.float32); a.snap_render_state()
        a.energy = a.MAX_ENERGY
    return ea, env

def say(env, tick_from=0):
    return [m for m in env.comm_bus.chat_archive if m.sent_tick >= tick_from]

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- T1: ĂĽgynĂ¶kĂ¶k Ă©s szerepek
ea, env = scenario(pop=24, mode="CLASSIC")
check("T1 minden robot kapott agentet, szerepet, profilt Ă©s ĂĽres Multi-Agent Ăˇllapotot",
      all(a.role_id in co.ROLES and a.profile is not None and a.commitment is None and a.awaiting_help is None for a in ea.agents))
cnt = collections.Counter(a.role_id for a in ea.agents)
check("T1 kiegyensĂşlyozott szerepek (van felderĂ­tĹ‘, tĂˇmogatĂł, vadĂˇsz, takarĂ­tĂł)", all(cnt[r] >= 2 for r in ("SCOUT","SUPPORT","HUNTER","CLEANER")), str(cnt))
check("T1 a szerep felirata lĂˇtszik a robot role mezĹ‘jĂ©ben", all(co.ROLE_LABEL[a.role_id] in a.role for a in ea.agents))

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- T2: cĂ©lpontmegosztĂˇs (TASK_ASSIGN -> elfogadĂˇs -> TARGET_CLAIM -> viselkedĂ©s)
ea, env = scenario(pop=5)
A, B, C = ea.agents[0], ea.agents[1], ea.agents[2]
A.set_role("SCOUT", 0); B.set_role("CLEANER", 0); C.set_role("CLEANER", 0)
for r in ea.agents[3:]: r.set_role("GENERAL", 0)
for dx in range(8):
    env.dirt_grid[44 + dx, 14] = 2
A.pos = np.array([44.0, 8.0], dtype=np.float32); B.pos = np.array([13.0, 8.0], dtype=np.float32); C.pos = np.array([30.0, 8.0], dtype=np.float32)
for a in (A, B, C): a.snap_render_state()
A.memory.remember_peer(B.id, B.name, B.pos, 100.0, 0, role="CLEANER")
for t in range(1, 12):
    env.step(ea.agents)
    if any(m.intent == "TARGET_CLAIM" and m.delivered_tick > 0 for m in env.comm_bus.chat_archive): break
msgs = say(env)
assigns = [m for m in msgs if m.intent == "TASK_ASSIGN"]
check("T2 a felderĂ­tĹ‘ strukturĂˇlt TASK_ASSIGN ĂĽzenetet kĂĽld a megfelelĹ‘ tĂˇrsnak", len(assigns) >= 1 and assigns[0].recipient_id == B.id and assigns[0].target_key.startswith("T"), str([m.intent for m in msgs]))
m0 = assigns[0] if assigns else None
check("T2 az ĂĽzenetet a cĂ­mzett tĂ©nylegesen feldolgozta (processed_by, processed_tick)", m0 is not None and B.id in m0.processed_by and m0.processed_tick >= 0)
check("T2 a cĂ­mzett elvĂˇllalta (ACCEPTED) Ă©s commitment keletkezett", m0 is not None and m0.status == "ACCEPTED" and B.commitment is not None and B.commitment["key"] == m0.target_key)
claims = [m for m in msgs if m.intent == "TARGET_CLAIM"]
check("T2 a cĂ­mzett TARGET_CLAIM-et kĂĽld a tĂ¶bbieknek", len(claims) >= 1 and claims[0].sender_id == B.id)
env.step(ea.agents)
check("T2 a kĂĽldĹ‘ tudomĂˇsul vette az ĂˇtvĂ©telt (az ajĂˇnlat lezĂˇrult)", m0 is not None and m0.target_key not in A.memory.sent_offers)
check("T2 a harmadik robot is tudja a foglalĂˇst (memĂłria)", m0 is not None and C.memory.target_claims.get(m0.target_key, (None,))[0] == B.id, str(C.memory.target_claims))
check("T2 a dĂ¶ntĂ©s tĂ©nyleg megvĂˇltozott: a vĂˇllalĂł az Ăˇtvett cĂ©lpont felĂ© halad", B.explanation.chosen_action.startswith("Ătvett cĂ©lpont"), B.explanation.chosen_action)
d0 = float(np.linalg.norm(B.commitment["pos"] - B.pos)) if B.commitment else 0.0
for t in range(8): env.step(ea.agents)
d1 = float(np.linalg.norm(B.commitment["pos"] - B.pos)) if B.commitment else 0.0
check("T2 a vĂˇllalĂł kĂ¶zeledik a cĂ©lponthoz", B.commitment is None or d1 < d0 - 3.0, f"{d0:.1f}->{d1:.1f}")
C.memory.remember_dirt(m0.pos + np.array([1.0, 0.0], dtype=np.float32), 5.0, env.tick_count)
spot = C.memory.best_unclaimed_dirt(C.pos, C.id)
check("T2 mĂˇs robot nem vĂˇlasztja a lefoglalt cĂ©lpontot emlĂ©kezetbĹ‘l", B.commitment is None or spot is None or float(np.linalg.norm(spot.pos - m0.pos)) > 6.0)
for t in range(60): env.step(ea.agents)
rel = [m for m in say(env) if m.intent == "TARGET_RELEASE"]
check("T2 teljesĂ­tĂ©skor TARGET_RELEASE megy ki Ă©s a foglalĂˇs felszabadul", len(rel) >= 1 and B.commitment is None and m0.target_key not in C.memory.target_claims, str([m.intent for m in say(env)]))

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- T3: elutasĂ­tĂˇs (alacsony energia) -> ACK(0) -> a kĂĽldĹ‘ mĂˇsikat keres
ea, env = scenario(pop=4)
A, B = ea.agents[0], ea.agents[1]
A.set_role("SCOUT", 0); B.set_role("CLEANER", 0)

for dx in range(8): env.dirt_grid[40 + dx, 12] = 2

for t in range(1, 8): env.step(ea.agents)
m = [x for x in say(env) if x.intent == "TASK_ASSIGN"]
check("T3 fĂˇradt cĂ­mzett elutasĂ­tja a feladatot (REJECTED) Ă©s nem vĂˇllal cĂ©lpontot", m and m[0].status == "REJECTED" and B.commitment is None, str([(x.intent, x.status) for x in say(env)]))
check("T3 az elutasĂ­tĂł visszajelzĂ©st (ACK) kap a kĂĽldĹ‘, Ă©s megjegyzi, ki utasĂ­totta el", B.id in A.memory.rejected_by)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- T4: segĂ©lykĂ©rĂ©s -> felajĂˇnlĂˇs -> visszaigazolĂˇs -> viselkedĂ©s (COOP Ă©s CLASSIC)
for mode in ("COOP", "CLASSIC"):
    ea, env = scenario(pop=3, mode=mode)
    A, B, C = ea.agents[0], ea.agents[1], ea.agents[2]
    B.set_role("SUPPORT", 0); C.set_role("SUPPORT", 0); A.set_role("GENERAL", 0)
    A.energy = 14.0
    A.pos = np.array([40.0, 8.0], dtype=np.float32); B.pos = np.array([30.0, 8.0], dtype=np.float32); C.pos = np.array([33.0, 9.0], dtype=np.float32)
    ever_helping = False
    for t in range(1, 30):
        env.step(ea.agents)
        ever_helping = ever_helping or any(x.state == 'HELPING_ALLY' or x.ally_target_pos is not None for x in (B, C))
        if any(m.intent == 'OFFER_ENERGY' for m in env.comm_bus.chat_archive) and any(m.intent == 'HELP_ACCEPTED' for m in env.comm_bus.chat_archive): break
    msgs = say(env)
    sos = [m for m in msgs if m.intent == "LOW_BAT_SOS"]
    offers = [m for m in msgs if m.intent == "OFFER_ENERGY"]
    acc = [m for m in msgs if m.intent == "HELP_ACCEPTED"]
    check(f"T4/{mode} a robot segĂ­tsĂ©get kĂ©r (LOW_BAT_SOS), a kĂ©rĹ‘ vĂˇrakozĂł Ăˇllapotba kerĂĽl", len(sos) >= 1 and (A.awaiting_help is not None))
    check(f"T4/{mode} a tĂˇrs elfogadta Ă©s tĂ©nylegesen mĂłdosĂ­totta a cĂ©ljĂˇt (ally_target)", len(offers) >= 1 and ever_helping, str([(m.intent, m.status) for m in msgs]))
    check(f"T4/{mode} a kĂ©rĹ‘ visszaigazol (HELP_ACCEPTED) az elsĹ‘ segĂ­tĹ‘nek", len(acc) >= 1)
    covered = [m for m in msgs if m.intent == "ACK" and m.target_key == "HELP_COVERED"]
    helpers_active = sum(1 for x in (B, C) if x.ally_target_pos is not None)
    check(f"T4/{mode} a mĂˇsodik segĂ­tĹ‘ nem megy feleslegesen (HELP_COVERED visszavonja)", helpers_active <= 1 or len(covered) >= 1, f"helpers={helpers_active} covered={len(covered)}")
    sp0 = A.explanation.chosen_action
    d_before = float(np.linalg.norm(A.pos - B.pos))
    for t in range(6, 40): env.step(ea.agents)
    d_after = float(np.linalg.norm(A.pos - B.pos))
    check(f"T4/{mode} a segĂ­tĹ‘ Ă©s a kĂ©rĹ‘ kĂ¶zeledik egymĂˇshoz, Ă©s energia ĂˇtadĂˇs tĂ¶rtĂ©nik", A.energy_received > 0 or d_after < d_before, f"{d_before:.1f}->{d_after:.1f} recv={A.energy_received:.1f}")

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- T5: veszĂ©lyjelzĂ©s (CLASSIC)
ea, env = scenario(pop=5, mode="CLASSIC")
H, V, W, S = ea.agents[0], ea.agents[1], ea.agents[2], ea.agents[3]
H.genome.genes[0] = 0.95; V.genome.genes[0] = 0.2
W.genome.genes[0] = 0.15      # gyenge/bĂ©kĂ©s tanĂş
S.genome.genes[0] = 0.9       # erĹ‘s/agresszĂ­v tanĂş
H.pos = np.array([40.0, 8.0], dtype=np.float32); V.pos = np.array([41.5, 8.0], dtype=np.float32)
W.pos = np.array([46.0, 9.0], dtype=np.float32); S.pos = np.array([36.0, 9.0], dtype=np.float32)
ea.agents[4].pos = np.array([70.0, 40.0], dtype=np.float32)
H.state = "HUNTING"; V.energy = 60.0
env._resolve_combat([a for a in ea.agents if a.alive])
check("T5 a megtĂˇmadott tudja, ki tĂˇmadta", V.last_attacker_id == H.id)
for t in range(1, 5): env.step(ea.agents)
thr = [m for m in say(env) if m.intent == "THREAT_DETECTED"]
check("T5 THREAT_DETECTED ĂĽzenet megy ki a tĂˇmadĂłrĂłl", len(thr) >= 1 and thr[0].target_key == f"R{H.id}", str([m.intent for m in say(env)]))
check("T5 a hatĂłtĂˇvon belĂĽli tanĂşk feldolgoztĂˇk Ă©s eltĂˇroltĂˇk a fenyegetĂ©st", all(H.id in x.memory.threats for x in (W, S)) and thr[0].status == "ACCEPTED")
check("T5 a tĂˇvoli robot nem kapta meg (hatĂłtĂˇvon kĂ­vĂĽl)", H.id not in ea.agents[4].memory.threats)
check("T5 a reakciĂł a tulajdonsĂˇgoktĂłl fĂĽgg (erĹ‘s: szembeszĂˇll, gyenge: kitĂ©r)", ("szembeszĂˇll" in thr[0].reaction_note or "kitĂ©r" in thr[0].reaction_note))
# viselkedĂ©s: a tanĂşk dĂ¶ntĂ©se a jelzĂ©s alapjĂˇn eltĂ©r
W.energy = 70.0; S.energy = 95.0
W.pos = np.array([42.0, 8.0], dtype=np.float32); S.pos = np.array([37.5, 8.0], dtype=np.float32)
H.pos = np.array([40.0, 8.0], dtype=np.float32)
for t in range(5, 9):
    for x in (W, S): x.memory.threats[H.id] = (H.pos.copy(), env.tick_count, 60.0)
    env.step(ea.agents)
check("T5 a gyenge tanĂş dĂ¶ntĂ©sĂ©ben megjelenik a veszĂ©lyjelzĂ©s (kitĂ©rĂ©s)", "VeszĂ©lyjelzĂ©s" in W.explanation.measurable_reason or W.explanation.chosen_action.startswith("MenekĂĽlĂ©s") or W.state == "FLEEING", W.explanation.measurable_reason)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- T6: elĂ©rhetetlen / kiesett robotok
ea, env = scenario(pop=4)
A, B, C = ea.agents[0], ea.agents[1], ea.agents[2]
B.set_role("CLEANER", 0)
A.memory.claim_target("T9:9", B.id, np.array([45.0, 45.0], dtype=np.float32), 0)
C.memory.claim_target("T9:9", B.id, np.array([45.0, 45.0], dtype=np.float32), 0)
A.ally_target_id, A.ally_target_pos = B.id, B.pos.copy()
A.memory.remember_peer(B.id, B.name, B.pos, 50.0, 0)
bus = env.comm_bus
spec = {"recipient_id": B.id, "recipient_label": B.name, "category": "[TASK]", "intent": "TASK_ASSIGN", "priority": 2, "pos": A.pos.copy(), "sector_id": 0, "value": 3.0, "text": "teszt", "target_key": "T1:1"}
B.alive = False; B.energy = 0.0
bus.enqueue_from_spec(A, spec, env.tick_count, env.rng)
for t in range(1, 5): env.step(ea.agents)
check("T6 kiesett cĂ­mzettnek kĂĽldĂ¶tt ĂĽzenet nem okoz hibĂˇt, elĂ©rhetetlenkĂ©nt jelĂ¶lt", bus.unreachable_count >= 1 or bus.chat_archive[-1].status in ("EXPIRED", "DROPPED"), str([(m.intent, m.status, m.reaction_note) for m in bus.chat_archive[-2:]]))
check("T6 a kiesett robot foglalĂˇsa felszabadult mindenkinĂ©l", "T9:9" not in A.memory.target_claims and "T9:9" not in C.memory.target_claims)
check("T6 a kiesett tĂˇrsra mutatĂł segĂ­tĹ‘-cĂ©l tĂ¶rlĹ‘dĂ¶tt", A.ally_target_id is None and B.id not in A.memory.peers)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- T7: spam-vĂ©delem
ea, env = scenario(pop=4)
A = ea.agents[0]
spec = {"recipient_id": None, "recipient_label": "TEAM", "category": "[TASK]", "intent": "TASK_OFFER", "priority": 2, "pos": A.pos.copy(), "sector_id": 0, "value": 3.0, "text": "x", "target_key": "T2:2"}
got = [env.comm_bus.enqueue_from_spec(A, dict(spec), 10, env.rng) for _ in range(30)]
check("T7 azonos ĂĽzenet ismĂ©tlĂ©se az idĹ‘ablakon belĂĽl elnyelĹ‘dik", sum(g is not None for g in got) == 1 and env.comm_bus.suppressed_duplicates == 29)
specs = [dict(spec, target_key=f"T{k}:0", intent="STATUS_UPDATE") for k in range(30)]
got2 = [env.comm_bus.enqueue_from_spec(A, s, 11, env.rng) for s in specs]
check("T7 tickenkĂ©nti sĂˇvszĂ©lessĂ©g-korlĂˇt (max 8 Ăşj alacsony prioritĂˇsĂş ĂĽzenet)", sum(g is not None for g in got2) <= 8 and env.comm_bus.suppressed_rate >= 20)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- T8: hosszĂş futĂˇs: szerepstabilitĂˇs, ĂĽzenetmennyisĂ©g, nincs hiba
for mode in ("CLASSIC", "COOP", "TDM"):
    ea, env = scenario(pop=24, mode=mode, seed=7)
    env.dirt_spawn_rate = 2
    env.spawn_dirt(120)
    role_hist = {a.id: [a.role_id] for a in ea.agents}
    changes_off_schedule = 0
    for t in range(600):
        before = {a.id: a.role_id for a in ea.agents}
        env.step(ea.agents)
        for a in ea.agents:
            if a.role_id != before[a.id] and env.tick_count % co.REBALANCE_INTERVAL != 0:
                changes_off_schedule += 1
    changed = sum(1 for a in ea.agents if a.role_id != role_hist[a.id][0])
    per_robot_per_100 = len(env.comm_bus.chat_archive) / 24 / 6.0
    check(f"T8/{mode} a szerepek stabilak (nincs ĂĽtemezĂ©sen kĂ­vĂĽli vĂˇltĂˇs, kevĂ©s vĂˇltĂˇs)", changes_off_schedule == 0 and changed <= 6, f"changed={changed}")
    check(f"T8/{mode} nincs ĂĽzenetĂˇradat (robotonkĂ©nt 100 tickre < 25 ĂĽzenet)", per_robot_per_100 < 25, f"{per_robot_per_100:.1f}")
    nonproc = [m for m in env.comm_bus.chat_archive if m.status in ("ACCEPTED", "REJECTED") and not m.processed_by]
    check(f"T8/{mode} minden ACCEPTED/REJECTED ĂĽzenetet valĂłban feldolgozott valaki", len(nonproc) == 0, str(len(nonproc)))

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- T9: ĂşjraindĂ­tĂˇs / lĂ©tszĂˇmvĂˇltozĂˇs
ea, env = scenario(pop=10, mode="COOP", seed=2)
for t in range(100): env.step(ea.agents)
env.reset(ea.agents)
check("T9 ĂşjraindĂ­tĂˇs: ĂĽzenetsor, archĂ­vum, foglalĂˇsok Ă©s megbĂ­zĂˇsok ĂĽresek", len(env.comm_bus.chat_archive) == 0 and all(a.commitment is None and a.awaiting_help is None and not a.inbox and not a.memory.target_claims for a in ea.agents))
ea2 = EvolutionaryAlgorithm(pop_size=14, mode="COOP"); env.reset(ea2.agents)
for t in range(50): env.step(ea2.agents)
check("T9 lĂ©tszĂˇmvĂˇltozĂˇs (10->14 robot) utĂˇn hibĂˇtlanul fut", len(ea2.agents) == 14 and all(a.role_id in co.ROLES for a in ea2.agents))
print("\nĂ–SSZESĂŤTĂ‰S:", "MINDEN TESZT SIKERES" if OK else "VAN HIBA")

=== FILE: C:\Users\dave\Desktop\egyetemgeci\5. felev\Projektmunka\project geminivel\3d verzio\archive\3d\tests\test_controller_stub.py ===
import sys, types, collections, os, json, math, tempfile
import os; sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))))

def _noop(*a, **k): return None
class StubEntity:
    count = 0; all = []
    rotation_x = rotation_y = rotation_z = 0.0
    def __init__(self, *a, **kw):
        StubEntity.count += 1; StubEntity.all.append(self)
        self.enabled = True; self.x = self.y = self.z = 0.0; self.scale = (1, 1, 1); self.text = ""; self.color = None
        for k, v in kw.items():
            if k == "position":
                v = tuple(v); self.x, self.y = v[0], v[1]; self.z = v[2] if len(v) > 2 else 0.0
            setattr(self, k, v)
        self.text_entity = types.SimpleNamespace(scale=1.0, color=None)
    @property
    def scale_x(self): return self.scale[0]
    @scale_x.setter
    def scale_x(self, v): self.scale = (v,) + tuple(self.scale[1:])
    @property
    def position(self): return (self.x, self.y, self.z)
    @position.setter
    def position(self, v): v = tuple(v); self.x, self.y = v[0], v[1]; self.z = v[2] if len(v) > 2 else self.z
    def __getattr__(self, n):
        if n.startswith("__"): raise AttributeError(n)
        return _noop
class CamStub(StubEntity):
    fov = 40.0; clip_plane_near = 0.1
    def __init__(self): super().__init__(); self.looks = []; self.ui = StubEntity()
    def look_at(self, t): self.looks.append(tuple(t))
class V3:
    def __init__(self, x=0, y=0, z=0): self.x, self.y, self.z = float(x), float(y), float(z)
    def __add__(s, o): return V3(s.x+o.x, s.y+o.y, s.z+o.z)
    def __sub__(s, o): return V3(s.x-o.x, s.y-o.y, s.z-o.z)
    def __mul__(s, k): return V3(s.x*k, s.y*k, s.z*k)
    __rmul__ = __mul__
    def length(s): return (s.x**2+s.y**2+s.z**2)**0.5
    def normalized(s): l = s.length() or 1; return V3(s.x/l, s.y/l, s.z/l)
    def __iter__(s): return iter((s.x, s.y, s.z))
    def __getitem__(s, i): return (s.x, s.y, s.z)[i]
class Color(tuple):
    def __new__(c, r, g, b, a=1.0): return tuple.__new__(c, (r, g, b, a))
m = types.ModuleType("ursina")
m.Entity = m.Text = m.Button = m.DirectionalLight = m.AmbientLight = StubEntity
m.Texture = lambda p: p; m.destroy = lambda e: None; m.Vec2 = lambda *a: a; m.Vec3 = V3
m.Ursina = lambda **k: None
m.color = types.SimpleNamespace(Color=Color, white=Color(1,1,1,1))
m.camera = CamStub()
class Mouse: x = 0.0; y = 0.0; velocity = (0.0, 0.0, 0.0); visible = True; locked = False
m.mouse = Mouse; m.held_keys = collections.defaultdict(int)
m.time = types.SimpleNamespace(dt=0.016)
class Win: aspect_ratio = 16/9; size = (1280, 720); fullscreen = False; color = None; screen_resolution = (1920, 1080); position = None
m.window = Win
sys.modules["ursina"] = m
sh = types.ModuleType("ursina.shaders"); sh.lit_with_shadows_shader = None; sys.modules["ursina.shaders"] = sh
