"""
Csapat-koordinĂˇciĂł Ă©s szerepek a helyben futĂł Multi-Agent rendszerhez (nincs LLM / hĂˇlĂłzat).

Tartalma:
  * ROLES / ROLE_PROFILES  - Ă¶t stabil szerep Ă©s a dĂ¶ntĂ©seket tĂ©nylegesen mĂłdosĂ­tĂł sĂşlyaik.
  * assign_roles()         - kezdeti szerepkiosztĂˇs a genombĂłl (+ minimĂˇlis csapatĂ¶sszetĂ©tel).
  * rebalance_roles()      - ritkĂˇn (60 tickenkĂ©nt) futĂł, indokolt szerepcsere, ha egy fontos szerep
                             (felderĂ­tĹ‘ / tĂˇmogatĂł) kiesett; csak zĂˇrolĂˇsi idĹ‘ lejĂˇrta utĂˇn.
  * dialogue_line()        - esemĂ©nyalapĂş szĂ¶vegsablonok a kommunikĂˇciĂłs naplĂłhoz / kĂ©sĹ‘bbi beszĂ©dbuborĂ©kokhoz.

A szerep NEM vĂ©letlen: a genom Ă©rtĂ©keibĹ‘l szĂˇmolĂłdik, `ROLE_MIN_HOLD` tickig zĂˇrolt, Ă©s csak a
`rebalance_roles` vĂˇltoztathat rajta, amikor tĂ©nylegesen hiĂˇnyzik egy szerep a csapatbĂłl.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Dict, Iterable, List, Optional

ROLES = ("SCOUT", "CLEANER", "SUPPORT", "HUNTER", "GENERAL")
ROLE_LABEL: Dict[str, str] = {
    "SCOUT": "FelderĂ­tĹ‘", "CLEANER": "TakarĂ­tĂł", "SUPPORT": "TĂˇmogatĂł", "HUNTER": "VadĂˇsz", "GENERAL": "ĂltalĂˇnos",
}
ROLE_SHORT: Dict[str, str] = {"SCOUT": "FEL", "CLEANER": "TAK", "SUPPORT": "TĂM", "HUNTER": "VAD", "GENERAL": "ĂLT"}

ROLE_MIN_HOLD: int = 150        # ennyi tickig nem vĂˇltozhat a szerep a kiosztĂˇs/csere utĂˇn
REBALANCE_INTERVAL: int = 60    # ennyi tickenkĂ©nt vizsgĂˇlja a koordinĂˇtor a csapatĂ¶sszetĂ©telt
COMBAT_MODES = ("CLASSIC", "TDM")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RoleProfile:
    """A szerep hatĂˇsa a robot dĂ¶ntĂ©seire (mind a decide_and_move()-ban hasznĂˇlt tĂ©nyleges szorzĂł/bĂłnusz)."""
    clean_bonus: float        # a takarĂ­tĂˇs hasznossĂˇgĂˇhoz adott pont
    help_bonus: float         # a segĂ­tsĂ©gnyĂşjtĂˇs hasznossĂˇgĂˇhoz adott pont
    hunt_mult: float          # a vadĂˇszat (kannibĂˇl tĂˇmadĂˇs) hasznossĂˇgĂˇnak szorzĂłja
    flee_mult: float          # a menekĂĽlĂ©s hasznossĂˇgĂˇnak szorzĂłja
    comm_cooldown_mult: float  # <1: gyakrabban kommunikĂˇl
    explore: bool             # frontier-alapĂş felderĂ­tĂ©st vĂ©gez, ha nincs cĂ©lpont
    share_threshold: int      # ennyi lĂˇtott szemĂ©tgĂłc fĂ¶lĂ¶tt osztja meg a cĂ©lpontot
    task_accept_bias: float   # a kapott feladat elfogadĂˇsi hajlandĂłsĂˇga (+: szĂ­vesebben vĂˇllal)
    help_energy_floor: float  # ennyi energia-tartalĂ©k (pont) kell a segĂ­tsĂ©gnyĂşjtĂˇshoz


ROLE_PROFILES: Dict[str, RoleProfile] = {
    "SCOUT":   RoleProfile(-6.0, 0.0, 0.80, 1.10, 0.60, True, 3, -0.35, 24.0),
    "CLEANER": RoleProfile(+9.0, 0.0, 0.85, 1.00, 1.00, False, 12, +0.30, 24.0),
    "SUPPORT": RoleProfile(0.0, +12.0, 0.70, 0.95, 0.85, False, 12, 0.0, 16.0),
    "HUNTER":  RoleProfile(-4.0, -6.0, 1.30, 0.80, 1.10, False, 14, -0.15, 30.0),
    "GENERAL": RoleProfile(0.0, 0.0, 1.00, 1.00, 1.00, False, 10, 0.0, 22.0),
}


def role_scores(genome, mode: str) -> Dict[str, float]:
    """Genom-alapĂş alkalmassĂˇgi pontszĂˇmok (0..~1.2)."""
    hunter = genome.aggressiveness * 1.15 if mode in COMBAT_MODES else 0.0
    return {
        "SCOUT": genome.exploration * 1.00 + (1.0 - genome.return_caution / 2.25) * 0.25,
        "CLEANER": (genome.dirt_affinity - 0.35) / 1.65 * 1.00,
        "SUPPORT": genome.cooperation * 1.10,
        "HUNTER": hunter,
    }


def pick_role(genome, mode: str, current: Optional[str] = None, margin: float = 0.08) -> str:
    """A legalkalmasabb szerep; ha nincs egyĂ©rtelmĹ± (margin) gyĹ‘ztes, ĂltalĂˇnos. HiszterĂ©zis a jelenlegi javĂˇra."""
    scores = role_scores(genome, mode)
    ranked = sorted(scores.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1], reverse=True)
    best, best_s = ranked[0]
    second_s = ranked[1][1] if len(ranked) > 1 else 0.0
    if best_s < 0.45 or best_s - second_s < margin:
        best = "GENERAL"
    if current and current != "GENERAL" and current in scores and scores[current] >= best_s - 0.12:
        return current          # kis kĂĽlĂ¶nbsĂ©gnĂ©l maradunk a jelenlegi szerepnĂ©l (nincs ide-oda ugrĂˇlĂˇs)
    return best


ROLE_QUOTA: Dict[str, float] = {"SCOUT": 0.20, "SUPPORT": 0.20, "HUNTER": 0.15, "CLEANER": 0.35}


def assign_roles(agents: Iterable, mode: str, tick: int = 0) -> None:
    """
    Kezdeti szerepkiosztĂˇs. Minden robot genom-alapĂş alkalmassĂˇgi pontszĂˇmot kap; a szerepeket a
    populĂˇciĂłn belĂĽli RELATĂŤV alkalmassĂˇg szerint, kvĂłtĂˇkkal osztjuk ki (kb. 20% felderĂ­tĹ‘, 20% tĂˇmogatĂł,
    15% vadĂˇsz [csak harci mĂłdban], 35% takarĂ­tĂł, a maradĂ©k ĂˇltalĂˇnos), Ă­gy mindig kiegyensĂşlyozott a csapat.
    A szerep ettĹ‘l fĂĽggetlenĂĽl a robot sajĂˇt gĂ©njeibĹ‘l kĂ¶vetkezik (a legalkalmasabbak kapjĂˇk).
    """
    agents = list(agents)
    n = len(agents)
    if n == 0:
        return
    for a in agents:
        a.set_role("GENERAL", tick, hold=ROLE_MIN_HOLD)
    if n < 4:
        for a in agents:
            a.set_role(pick_role(a.genome, mode), tick, hold=ROLE_MIN_HOLD)
        return
    scores = {a.id: role_scores(a.genome, mode) for a in agents}
    order = ["SCOUT", "SUPPORT"] + (["HUNTER"] if mode in COMBAT_MODES else []) + ["CLEANER"]
    free = list(agents)
    for role in order:
        quota = max(1, int(round(ROLE_QUOTA[role] * n)))
        mean = sum(scores[a.id][role] for a in agents) / n
        ranked = sorted(free, key=lambda a: scores[a.id][role] - mean, reverse=True)
        for a in ranked[:quota]:
            a.set_role(role, tick, hold=ROLE_MIN_HOLD)
            free.remove(a)


def _team_groups(agents: List, mode: str) -> List[List]:
    if mode == "TDM":
        teams: Dict[int, List] = {}
        for a in agents:
            teams.setdefault(a.team_id, []).append(a)
        return list(teams.values())
    return [agents]


def _ensure_role(agents: List, role: str, mode: str, tick: int) -> None:
    for group in _team_groups(agents, mode):
        if len(group) < 4:
            continue
        if any(a.role_id == role and a.alive for a in group):
            continue
        candidates = [a for a in group if a.alive and a.role_id in ("GENERAL", "CLEANER", "HUNTER", "SCOUT", "SUPPORT")]
        if not candidates:
            continue
        # a legjobb jelĂ¶lt, de nem vesszĂĽk el egy mĂˇsik hiĂˇnyzĂł szerep egyetlen birtokosĂˇtĂłl
        def keep_penalty(a) -> float:
            same = sum(1 for b in group if b.role_id == a.role_id and b.alive)
            return 0.0 if same > 1 or a.role_id == "GENERAL" else 1.0
        best = max(candidates, key=lambda a: role_scores(a.genome, mode).get(role, 0.0) - keep_penalty(a))
        best.set_role(role, tick, hold=ROLE_MIN_HOLD)


def rebalance_roles(agents: List, mode: str, tick: int) -> List[str]:
    """
    Ritka (REBALANCE_INTERVAL) csapatĂ¶sszetĂ©tel-ellenĹ‘rzĂ©s: ha egy fontos szerep birtokosa kiesett,
    a legalkalmasabb, zĂˇrolĂˇsĂˇtĂłl mentes robot Ăˇtveszi. Visszaadja a vĂˇltozĂˇsok leĂ­rĂˇsĂˇt.
    """
    if tick % REBALANCE_INTERVAL != 0:
        return []
    changes: List[str] = []
    living = [a for a in agents if a.alive]
    if len(living) < 4:
        return changes
    for group in _team_groups(living, mode):
        for role in (("SCOUT", "SUPPORT", "HUNTER") if mode in COMBAT_MODES else ("SCOUT", "SUPPORT")):
            if any(a.role_id == role for a in group) or len(group) < 4:
                continue
            free = [a for a in group if tick >= a.role_lock_until and a.role_id in ("GENERAL", "CLEANER")]
            if not free:
                continue
            cand = max(free, key=lambda a: role_scores(a.genome, mode).get(role, 0.0))
            if role_scores(cand.genome, mode).get(role, 0.0) < 0.25:
                continue
            old = cand.role_id
            cand.set_role(role, tick, hold=ROLE_MIN_HOLD)
            changes.append(f"{cand.name}: {ROLE_LABEL[old]} -> {ROLE_LABEL[role]} (a csapatbĂłl hiĂˇnyzott)")
    return changes


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- szĂ¶vegsablonok
def dialogue_line(msg, role_of: Optional[Dict[int, str]] = None) -> str:
    """
    Egy ĂĽzenet rĂ¶vid, esemĂ©nyalapĂş megjelenĂ­tĂ©si sora: "R-07 (FEL) -> R-12: szĂ¶veg".
    A szĂ¶veg az ĂĽzenet strukturĂˇlt mezĹ‘ibĹ‘l (intent, status, reaction_note) Ăˇll Ă¶ssze, ezĂ©rt csak
    valĂłban megtĂ¶rtĂ©nt esemĂ©nyt jelenĂ­t meg.
    """
    role = ROLE_SHORT.get(getattr(msg, "sender_role", "") or (role_of or {}).get(msg.sender_id, ""), "")
    who = f"{msg.sender_name}({role})" if role else msg.sender_name
    note = f" [{msg.reaction_note}]" if getattr(msg, "reaction_note", "") else ""
    return f"{who}->{msg.recipient_label}: {msg.text}{note}"

import math
import csv
import json
import os
from datetime import datetime
import numpy as np
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List, Tuple, Dict, Any, Optional
from agent import RobotAgent, AgentMessage
import coordination


@dataclass
class EventLogEntry:
    """RendszerszintĹ± Ă©s fizikai esemĂ©nynaplĂł-bejegyzĂ©s."""
    tick: int
    agent_name: str
    category: str  # "TAKARĂŤTĂS", "ENERGIA", "HARC", "KĂśLDETĂ‰S"
    message: str


def extract_merged_rectangles(grid: np.ndarray, target_val: int) -> List[Tuple[int, int, int, int]]:
    """
    Greedy Meshing (2D tĂ©glalap-Ă¶sszevonĂł) algoritmus a 3D-s Ursina pĂˇlyĂˇkhoz.
    Az Ă¶sszefĂĽggĹ‘ azonos tĂ­pusĂş rĂˇcscellĂˇkat (falakat, ĂˇllvĂˇnyokat, padlĂłzĂłnĂˇkat)
    minimĂˇlis szĂˇmĂş (x0, y0, w, h) tĂ©glalappĂˇ vonja Ă¶ssze, Ă­gy 5000+ kocka helyett
    mindĂ¶ssze ~40 optimalizĂˇlt 3D-s objektumot kell renderelni!
    """
    w_grid, h_grid = grid.shape
    visited = np.zeros((w_grid, h_grid), dtype=bool)
    rects: List[Tuple[int, int, int, int]] = []

    for x in range(w_grid):
        for y in range(h_grid):
            if grid[x, y] == target_val and not visited[x, y]:
                rw = 1
                while x + rw < w_grid and grid[x + rw, y] == target_val and not visited[x + rw, y]:
                    rw += 1

                rh = 1
                can_expand = True
                while y + rh < h_grid and can_expand:
                    for ix in range(x, x + rw):
                        if grid[ix, y + rh] != target_val or visited[ix, y + rh]:
                            can_expand = False
                            break
                    if can_expand:
                        rh += 1

                visited[x : x + rw, y : y + rh] = True
                rects.append((x, y, rw, rh))

    return rects


class CommunicationBus:
    """DedikĂˇlt kommunikĂˇciĂłs rĂ©teg a robotok kĂ¶zĂ¶tti ĂĽzenetkĂĽldĂ©shez Ă©s archivĂˇlĂˇshoz."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        comm_range: float = 38.0,
        delivery_delay_ticks: int = 1,
        packet_loss_rate: float = 0.02,
    ):
        self.comm_range: float = comm_range
        self.delivery_delay_ticks: int = delivery_delay_ticks
        self.packet_loss_rate: float = packet_loss_rate
        self.enabled: bool = True

        self.next_seq: int = 1
        self.pending_queue: List[AgentMessage] = []
        self.chat_archive: List[AgentMessage] = []
        self.delivered_this_tick: List[AgentMessage] = []
        # --- spam-vĂ©delem: azonos ĂĽzenet ismĂ©tlĂ©sĂ©nek tiltĂˇsa idĹ‘ablakon belĂĽl + tickenkĂ©nti sĂˇvszĂ©lessĂ©g-korlĂˇt
        self._recent: Dict[tuple, int] = {}
        self._tick_counter: Tuple[int, int] = (-1, 0)
        self.max_new_per_tick: int = 8
        self.suppressed_duplicates: int = 0
        self.suppressed_rate: int = 0
        self.unreachable_count: int = 0

    DEDUPE_WINDOW: Dict[str, int] = {
        "TASK_ASSIGN": 30, "TASK_OFFER": 30, "TARGET_CLAIM": 20, "TARGET_RELEASE": 10, "DIRT_SPOT": 25,
        "LOW_BAT_SOS": 40, "THREAT_DETECTED": 20, "STATUS_UPDATE": 30,
    }

    def reset(self) -> None:
        self.next_seq = 1
        self.pending_queue.clear()
        self.chat_archive.clear()
        self.delivered_this_tick.clear()
        self._recent.clear()
        self._tick_counter = (-1, 0)
        self.suppressed_duplicates = 0
        self.suppressed_rate = 0
        self.unreachable_count = 0

    def history_for(self, agent_id: int, limit: int = 20) -> List[AgentMessage]:
        """Egy robot kommunikĂˇciĂłs elĹ‘zmĂ©nye (kĂĽldĂ¶tt, neki cĂ­mzett Ă©s Ăˇltala feldolgozott ĂĽzenetek) - kĂ©sĹ‘bbi panelekhez."""
        out = [m for m in self.chat_archive
               if m.sender_id == agent_id or m.recipient_id == agent_id or agent_id in m.processed_by]
        return out[-limit:]

    def thread(self, thread_id: str) -> List[AgentMessage]:
        """Egy kĂ©rĂ©s-vĂˇlasz lĂˇnc (tĂ¶bbfordulĂłs pĂˇrbeszĂ©dekhez)."""
        return [m for m in self.chat_archive if m.thread_id == thread_id]

    def enqueue_from_spec(
        self, sender: RobotAgent, spec: Dict[str, Any], current_tick: int, rng: np.random.Generator
    ) -> Optional[AgentMessage]:
        # ---- duplikĂˇciĂł- Ă©s sĂˇvszĂ©lessĂ©g-szĹ±rĂ©s (None = az ĂĽzenet nem kerĂĽl sorba)
        intent = spec.get("intent", "DIRT_SPOT")
        p = spec["pos"]
        tk = spec.get("target_key") or f"{int(p[0] // 6)}:{int(p[1] // 6)}:{spec.get('sector_id', 0)}"
        dkey = (sender.id, intent, spec.get("recipient_id"), tk, spec.get("ref_msg_id") or "")
        window = self.DEDUPE_WINDOW.get(intent, 15)
        last = self._recent.get(dkey)
        if last is not None and current_tick - last < window:
            self.suppressed_duplicates += 1
            return None
        t_now, n_now = self._tick_counter
        if t_now != current_tick:
            t_now, n_now = current_tick, 0
        if n_now >= self.max_new_per_tick and int(spec.get("priority", 1)) < 3:
            self.suppressed_rate += 1
            self._tick_counter = (t_now, n_now)
            return None
        self._tick_counter = (t_now, n_now + 1)
        self._recent[dkey] = current_tick
        if len(self._recent) > 4000:
            self._recent = {k: t for k, t in self._recent.items() if current_tick - t < 60}

        msg_id = f"MSG-{self.next_seq:04d}"
        self.next_seq += 1

        msg = AgentMessage(
            msg_id=msg_id,
            sender_id=sender.id,
            sender_name=sender.name,
            team_id=sender.team_id,
            recipient_id=spec.get("recipient_id", None),
            recipient_label=spec.get("recipient_label", "TEAM"),
            category=spec.get("category", "[INFO]"),
            intent=spec.get("intent", "DIRT_SPOT"),
            priority=int(spec.get("priority", 1)),
            sent_tick=current_tick,
            deliver_tick=current_tick + self.delivery_delay_ticks,
            ttl=30,
            pos=spec["pos"].copy(),
            sector_id=int(spec.get("sector_id", 0)),
            value=float(spec.get("value", 0.0)),
            text=spec["text"],
            ref_msg_id=spec.get("ref_msg_id", None),
            status="QUEUED",
            sender_role=getattr(sender, "role_id", ""),
            target_key=spec.get("target_key", ""),
            origin=sender.pos.copy(),
        )
        ref = spec.get("ref_msg_id")
        msg.thread_id = spec.get("thread_id") or (ref if ref else msg_id)

        sender.messages_sent += 1
        if rng.random() < self.packet_loss_rate:
            msg.status = "DROPPED"
            msg.reaction_note = "RĂˇdiĂłjel elveszett (interferencia)"
            self.chat_archive.append(msg)
        else:
            self.pending_queue.append(msg)
            self.chat_archive.append(msg)

        if len(self.chat_archive) > 600:
            self.chat_archive.pop(0)

        return msg

    def process_deliveries(
        self, current_tick: int, living_agents: List[RobotAgent], mode: str
    ) -> List[AgentMessage]:
        self.delivered_this_tick.clear()
        if not self.enabled:
            return []

        remaining_queue: List[AgentMessage] = []
        for msg in self.pending_queue:
            if current_tick - msg.sent_tick > msg.ttl:
                msg.status = "EXPIRED"
                continue

            if current_tick < msg.deliver_tick:
                remaining_queue.append(msg)
                continue

            delivered_count = 0
            addressee_seen = msg.recipient_id is None
            origin = msg.origin if msg.origin is not None else msg.pos
            for receiver in living_agents:
                if receiver.id == msg.sender_id or not receiver.alive:
                    continue
                if mode == "TDM" and receiver.team_id != msg.team_id:
                    continue
                if msg.recipient_id is not None and receiver.id != msg.recipient_id:
                    continue
                addressee_seen = True
                dist = float(np.linalg.norm(receiver.pos - origin))
                if dist <= self.comm_range:
                    receiver.receive_message(msg)
                    delivered_count += 1

            if delivered_count > 0:
                msg.status = "DELIVERED"
                msg.delivered_tick = current_tick
                self.delivered_this_tick.append(msg)
            else:
                msg.status = "EXPIRED"
                if not addressee_seen:
                    msg.reaction_note = "CĂ­mzett elĂ©rhetetlen (kiesett / nincs a csapatban)"
                    self.unreachable_count += 1
                else:
                    msg.reaction_note = "HatĂłtĂˇvon kĂ­vĂĽl"

        self.pending_queue = remaining_queue
        return self.delivered_this_tick

    def export_chat_log(self, mode: str, generation: int) -> str:
        if not self.chat_archive:
            return "A Chat Log mĂ©g ĂĽres!"

        stamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
        base_name = f"chatlog_{mode.lower()}_gen{generation}_{stamp}"
        json_path = os.path.abspath(f"{base_name}.json")
        csv_path = os.path.abspath(f"{base_name}.csv")

        records = []
        for m in self.chat_archive:
            records.append({
                "msg_id": m.msg_id,
                "sent_tick": m.sent_tick,
                "deliver_tick": m.deliver_tick,
                "sender": m.sender_name,
                "recipient": m.recipient_label,
                "team_id": m.team_id,
                "category": m.category,
                "intent": m.intent,
                "priority": m.priority,
                "sector_id": m.sector_id + 1,
                "status": m.status,
                "reaction": m.reaction_note,
                "text": m.text,
            })

        with open(json_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as jf:
            json.dump({"mode": mode, "generation": generation, "messages": records}, jf, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)

        with open(csv_path, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as cf:
            writer = csv.DictWriter(cf, fieldnames=list(records[0].keys()))
            writer.writeheader()
            writer.writerows(records)

        return f"Chat mentve: {os.path.basename(json_path)} & .csv"

        "name": "3. Complex Apartment",

        "desc": "TĂ¶bb helyisĂ©gbĹ‘l, ajtĂłkeretekbĹ‘l, szĹ‘nyegekbĹ‘l Ă©s bĂştorokbĂłl ĂˇllĂł lakĂłtĂ©r.",
        "theme": "APARTMENT",

        "wall_tex": "wall_brick.png",
        "rack_tex": "rack_metal.png",
    },
    "ANTARCTIC": {

        "difficulty": "ExtrĂ©m ***",
        "desc": "Jeges kutatĂłbĂˇzis A indulĂˇsi Ă©s B cĂ©lĂˇllomĂˇssal, tĂ¶ltĹ‘dokk nĂ©lkĂĽl, fagyzĂłnĂˇkkal.",
        "theme": "ANTARCTIC",

    "INDUSTRIAL": {

        "theme": "INDUSTRIAL",

        "floor_tex": "floor_arena.png",
        "wall_tex": "wall_hazard.png",
        "rack_tex": "rack_metal.png",
    },
}


MAX_WORLD_SCALE: int = 5   # 5 x 5 csempe = a sablon 25-szĂ¶rĂ¶s terĂĽlete


class SimulationEnvironment:
    """
    OptimalizĂˇlt szimulĂˇciĂłs kĂ¶rnyezet elĹ‘re kiszĂˇmĂ­tott taszĂ­tĂł vektormezĹ‘kkel
    Ă©s 3D-s Greedy-Mesh geometria-exportĂˇlĂˇssal.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        width: int = 88,
        height: int = 60,
        initial_dirt: int = 130,
        dirt_spawn_rate: int = 2,
        map_key: str = "COMPACT",
        mode: str = "CLASSIC",
        seed: int = 42,
        world_scale: int = 1,
    ):
        # A `width`/`height` a pĂˇlya-SABLON mĂ©rete; a vilĂˇg ennek `world_scale` x `world_scale`-szoros
        # csempĂ©zĂ©se (lineĂˇris skĂˇla; a terĂĽlet world_scale^2-szeresĂ©re nĹ‘).
        self.world_scale: int = max(1, min(MAX_WORLD_SCALE, int(world_scale)))
        self.tile_w: int = width
        self.tile_h: int = height
        self.width: int = width * self.world_scale
        self.height: int = height * self.world_scale
        area_factor = self.world_scale ** 2
        self.initial_dirt: int = initial_dirt * area_factor
        self.dirt_spawn_rate: int = dirt_spawn_rate * area_factor if dirt_spawn_rate > 0 else 0
        self.map_key: str = map_key if map_key in MAP_CATALOG else "COMPACT"
        self.mode: str = mode
        self.seed: int = seed
        self.rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

        self.dirt_grid: np.ndarray = np.zeros((self.width, self.height), dtype=np.int8)
        self.obstacle_grid: np.ndarray = np.zeros((self.width, self.height), dtype=np.int8)
        self.floor_grid: np.ndarray = np.zeros((self.width, self.height), dtype=np.int8)
        self.hazard_grid: np.ndarray = np.zeros((self.width, self.height), dtype=np.int8)

        self.wall_field: np.ndarray = np.zeros((self.width, self.height, 2), dtype=np.float32)
        self.hazard_field: np.ndarray = np.zeros((self.width, self.height, 2), dtype=np.float32)

        self.chargers: List[np.ndarray] = []
        self.base_pos: np.ndarray = np.array([10.0, self.height / 2.0], dtype=np.float32)
        self.goal_pos: np.ndarray = np.array([self.width - 10.0, self.height / 2.0], dtype=np.float32)

        # A rĂˇdiĂł hatĂłtĂˇvja a vilĂˇggal egyĂĽtt (mĂ©rsĂ©kelten) nĹ‘, hogy nagy pĂˇlyĂˇn se legyenek teljesen elszigeteltek a robotok
        self.comm_bus: CommunicationBus = CommunicationBus(comm_range=min(80.0, 38.0 * (1.0 + 0.25 * (self.world_scale - 1))))
        self._template: Optional["SimulationEnvironment"] = None
        if self.world_scale > 1:
            self._template = SimulationEnvironment(
                width=self.tile_w, height=self.tile_h, initial_dirt=0, dirt_spawn_rate=0,
                map_key=self.map_key, mode=self.mode, seed=seed, world_scale=1,
            )

        self.combat_events: List[Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]] = []
        self.energy_share_events: List[Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]] = []
        self.comm_events: List[AgentMessage] = []
        self.charge_events: List[int] = []
        self.role_events: List[str] = []
        self._dead_notified: set = set()
        self.event_log: List[EventLogEntry] = []

        self.tick_count: int = 0
        self.step_kills: int = 0
        self.total_dirt_spawned: int = 0
        self.total_dirt_cleaned: int = 0
        self.total_energy_transfers: int = 0
        self.total_energy_shared_amount: float = 0.0
        self.total_messages: int = 0
        self.last_step_ai_decisions: int = 0

        self._build_map(self.map_key)

    @property
    def communication_enabled(self) -> bool:
        return self.comm_bus.enabled

    @communication_enabled.setter
    def communication_enabled(self, val: bool) -> None:
        self.comm_bus.enabled = val

    def add_log(self, tick: int, agent_name: str, category: str, message: str) -> None:
        self.event_log.append(EventLogEntry(tick=tick, agent_name=agent_name, category=category, message=message))
        if len(self.event_log) > 250:
            self.event_log.pop(0)

    @staticmethod
    def _repulsion_field(mask: np.ndarray, radius: int, power: float, min_dist: float) -> np.ndarray:
        """
        TaszĂ­tĂł vektormezĹ‘ az `mask > 0` cellĂˇk kĂ¶rĂĽl (a cella-kĂ¶zĂ©ppontok kĂ¶zti (diff / dist^power)
        Ă¶sszeg, normalizĂˇlva). EgyenĂ©rtĂ©kĹ± a korĂˇbbi cellĂˇnkĂ©nti Python-ciklussal, de a (2r+1)^2
        eltolĂˇs Ă¶sszegzĂ©sĂ©vel vektorizĂˇlt, ezĂ©rt a 25-szĂ¶rĂ¶s terĂĽlet is gyorsan szĂˇmolhatĂł.
        """
        w, h = mask.shape
        occ = (mask > 0).astype(np.float32)
        padded = np.pad(occ, radius)
        fx = np.zeros((w, h), dtype=np.float32)
        fy = np.zeros((w, h), dtype=np.float32)
        for dx in range(-radius, radius + 1):
            for dy in range(-radius, radius + 1):
                if dx == 0 and dy == 0:
                    continue
                dist = max(math.hypot(dx, dy), min_dist)
                # a (x, y) cellĂˇhoz kĂ©pest a (x+dx, y+dy) akadĂˇly: diff = pos - coords = (-dx, -dy)
                shifted = padded[radius + dx: radius + dx + w, radius + dy: radius + dy + h]
                k = 1.0 / (dist ** (power + 1.0)) if power else 1.0 / dist
                fx -= dx * k * shifted
                fy -= dy * k * shifted
        norm = np.sqrt(fx * fx + fy * fy)
        out = np.zeros((w, h, 2), dtype=np.float32)
        ok = norm > 1e-5
        out[..., 0][ok] = fx[ok] / norm[ok]
        out[..., 1][ok] = fy[ok] / norm[ok]
        return out

    def _precompute_vector_fields(self) -> None:
        # fal-taszĂ­tĂˇs: 7x7 ablak, 1/d^3 erĹ‘, min. tĂˇvolsĂˇg 0.3; veszĂ©lyzĂłna: 9x9 ablak, 1/d^2 erĹ‘, min. 0.5
        self.wall_field = self._repulsion_field(self.obstacle_grid, 3, 2.0, 0.3)
        self.hazard_field = self._repulsion_field(self.hazard_grid, 4, 1.0, 0.5)

    def _precompute_vector_fields_reference(self) -> None:
        """A korĂˇbbi, lassĂş, cellĂˇnkĂ©nti referencia-implementĂˇciĂł (csak tesztelĂ©sre)."""
        self.wall_field = np.zeros((self.width, self.height, 2), dtype=np.float32)
        self.hazard_field = np.zeros((self.width, self.height, 2), dtype=np.float32)
        for x in range(self.width):
            for y in range(self.height):
                pos = np.array([x + 0.5, y + 0.5], dtype=np.float32)
                x0, x1 = max(0, x - 3), min(self.width, x + 4)
                y0, y1 = max(0, y - 3), min(self.height, y + 4)
                sub_obs = self.obstacle_grid[x0:x1, y0:y1]
                obs_idx = np.argwhere(sub_obs > 0)
                if len(obs_idx) > 0:
                    coords = obs_idx + np.array([x0, y0], dtype=np.float32) + 0.5
                    diffs = pos - coords
                    dists = np.maximum(np.linalg.norm(diffs, axis=1, keepdims=True), 0.3)
                    forces = (diffs / (dists ** 3)).sum(axis=0)
                    norm = float(np.linalg.norm(forces))
                    if norm > 1e-5:
                        self.wall_field[x, y] = forces / norm
                hx0, hx1 = max(0, x - 4), min(self.width, x + 5)
                hy0, hy1 = max(0, y - 4), min(self.height, y + 5)
                sub_hz = self.hazard_grid[hx0:hx1, hy0:hy1]
                hz_idx = np.argwhere(sub_hz > 0)
                if len(hz_idx) > 0:
                    coords = hz_idx + np.array([hx0, hy0], dtype=np.float32) + 0.5
                    diffs = pos - coords
                    dists = np.maximum(np.linalg.norm(diffs, axis=1, keepdims=True), 0.5)
                    forces = (diffs / (dists ** 2)).sum(axis=0)
                    norm = float(np.linalg.norm(forces))
                    if norm > 1e-5:
                        self.hazard_field[x, y] = forces / norm

    def _build_map(self, map_key: str) -> None:
        if self.world_scale > 1 and self._template is not None:
            self._assemble_tiled_world()
            return
        self._build_template_map(map_key)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ nagy pĂˇlyĂˇk (csempĂ©zĂ©s)
    @staticmethod
    def _tile_row(template_y: float, tile_h: int, k: int) -> int:
        """Melyik csempe-sorba kerĂĽl a bĂˇzis/cĂ©l: a sablonbeli fĂĽggĹ‘leges helyzete szerint."""
        if template_y < tile_h * 0.35:
            return 0
        if template_y > tile_h * 0.65:
            return k - 1
        return k // 2

    def _assemble_tiled_world(self) -> None:
        """
        A `world_scale` x `world_scale` csempĂ©bĹ‘l ĂˇllĂł nagy vilĂˇg Ă©pĂ­tĂ©se a sablonpĂˇlyĂˇbĂłl:
          * a csempĂ©k a szomszĂ©dos sorban/oszlopban tĂĽkrĂ¶zve ismĂ©tlĹ‘dnek (vĂˇltozatosabb elrendezĂ©s),
          * a csempĂ©k kĂ¶zti falakon ajtĂłnyĂ­lĂˇsok vannak (3 / csempe-oldal), Ă­gy a vilĂˇg Ă¶sszefĂĽggĹ‘,
          * a bĂˇzis a bal oldali, a cĂ©l a jobb oldali csempĂ©ben van, a tĂ¶ltĹ‘k minden csempĂ©ben,
          * a bĂˇzis-/cĂ©l-padlĂł csak a sajĂˇt csempĂ©jĂ©ben marad.
        """
        t = self._template
        assert t is not None
        k, tw, th = self.world_scale, self.tile_w, self.tile_h
        self.obstacle_grid.fill(0)
        self.floor_grid.fill(0)
        self.hazard_grid.fill(0)
        self.chargers = []

        base_row = self._tile_row(float(t.base_pos[1]), th, k)
        goal_row = self._tile_row(float(t.goal_pos[1]), th, k)

        for i in range(k):
            for j in range(k):
                is_base = (i == 0 and j == base_row)
                is_goal = (i == k - 1 and j == goal_row)
                flip_x = (i % 2 == 1) and not is_goal
                flip_y = (j % 2 == 1) and not (is_base or is_goal)
                obs, flo, haz = t.obstacle_grid, t.floor_grid.copy(), t.hazard_grid
                if flip_x:
                    obs, flo, haz = obs[::-1, :], flo[::-1, :], haz[::-1, :]
                if flip_y:
                    obs, flo, haz = obs[:, ::-1], flo[:, ::-1], haz[:, ::-1]
                flo = flo.copy()
                if not is_base:
                    flo[flo == 1] = 0
                if not is_goal:
                    flo[flo == 2] = 0
                x0, y0 = i * tw, j * th
                self.obstacle_grid[x0:x0 + tw, y0:y0 + th] = obs
                self.floor_grid[x0:x0 + tw, y0:y0 + th] = flo
                self.hazard_grid[x0:x0 + tw, y0:y0 + th] = haz
                for c in t.chargers:
                    cx = (tw - c[0]) if flip_x else c[0]
                    cy = (th - c[1]) if flip_y else c[1]
                    self.chargers.append(np.array([x0 + cx, y0 + cy], dtype=np.float32))

        # ajtĂłnyĂ­lĂˇsok a csempĂ©k kĂ¶zĂ¶tti (dupla) falakon; az ajtĂł kĂ¶rĂĽli 8x8-as zĂłnĂˇt szabaddĂˇ tesszĂĽk
        for s in range(1, k):
            sx, sy = s * tw, s * th
            for t_idx in range(k):
                for frac in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75):
                    yc = int(t_idx * th + th * frac)
                    self.obstacle_grid[sx - 4: sx + 4, yc - 4: yc + 4] = 0
                    self.hazard_grid[sx - 4: sx + 4, yc - 4: yc + 4] = 0
                    xc = int(t_idx * tw + tw * frac)
                    self.obstacle_grid[xc - 4: xc + 4, sy - 4: sy + 4] = 0
                    self.hazard_grid[xc - 4: xc + 4, sy - 4: sy + 4] = 0

        # a kĂĽlsĹ‘ keret mindig fal
        self.obstacle_grid[0, :] = 1
        self.obstacle_grid[-1, :] = 1
        self.obstacle_grid[:, 0] = 1
        self.obstacle_grid[:, -1] = 1

        self.base_pos = np.array([t.base_pos[0], base_row * th + t.base_pos[1]], dtype=np.float32)
        self.goal_pos = np.array([(k - 1) * tw + t.goal_pos[0], goal_row * th + t.goal_pos[1]], dtype=np.float32)
        if self.mode == "ANTARCTIC":
            self.chargers = []
        self._precompute_vector_fields()

    def _build_template_map(self, map_key: str) -> None:
        self.obstacle_grid.fill(0)
        self.floor_grid.fill(0)
        self.hazard_grid.fill(0)
        self.chargers.clear()

        w, h = self.width, self.height
        self.obstacle_grid[0, :] = 1
        self.obstacle_grid[-1, :] = 1
        self.obstacle_grid[:, 0] = 1
        self.obstacle_grid[:, -1] = 1

        if map_key == "COMPACT":
            pillars = [
                (w // 4, h // 4, 4, 8),
                (3 * w // 4 - 4, h // 4, 4, 8),
                (w // 4, 3 * h // 4 - 8, 4, 8),
                (3 * w // 4 - 4, 3 * h // 4 - 8, 4, 8),
                (w // 2 - 6, h // 2 - 2, 12, 4),
            ]
            for px, py, pw, ph in pillars:
                self.obstacle_grid[px : px + pw, py : py + ph] = 1

            self.base_pos = np.array([10.0, h / 2.0], dtype=np.float32)
            self.goal_pos = np.array([w - 10.0, h / 2.0], dtype=np.float32)
            self.floor_grid[4:16, h // 2 - 8 : h // 2 + 8] = 1
            self.chargers = [
                np.array([10.0, 10.0], dtype=np.float32),
                np.array([w - 10.0, h - 10.0], dtype=np.float32),
            ]

        elif map_key == "WAREHOUSE":
            for col_x in range(18, w - 16, 14):
                self.obstacle_grid[col_x : col_x + 3, 7 : h // 2 - 4] = 2
                self.obstacle_grid[col_x : col_x + 3, h // 2 + 4 : h - 7] = 2
            self.base_pos = np.array([8.0, h / 2.0], dtype=np.float32)
            self.goal_pos = np.array([w - 8.0, h / 2.0], dtype=np.float32)
            self.floor_grid[2:14, h // 2 - 10 : h // 2 + 10] = 1
            self.floor_grid[w - 14 : w - 2, h // 2 - 10 : h // 2 + 10] = 2
            self.chargers = [
                np.array([8.0, 8.0], dtype=np.float32),
                np.array([8.0, h - 8.0], dtype=np.float32),
            ]

        elif map_key == "APARTMENT":
            mid_x, mid_y = w // 2, h // 2
            self.obstacle_grid[mid_x - 1 : mid_x + 1, 1 : h - 1] = 1
            self.obstacle_grid[1 : w - 1, mid_y - 1 : mid_y + 1] = 1

            self.obstacle_grid[mid_x - 1 : mid_x + 1, mid_y - 13 : mid_y - 6] = 0
            self.obstacle_grid[mid_x - 1 : mid_x + 1, mid_y + 6 : mid_y + 13] = 0
            self.obstacle_grid[mid_x - 18 : mid_x - 11, mid_y - 1 : mid_y + 1] = 0
            self.obstacle_grid[mid_x + 11 : mid_x + 18, mid_y - 1 : mid_y + 1] = 0

            self.floor_grid[6 : mid_x - 4, 6 : mid_y - 4] = 3
            self.floor_grid[mid_x + 4 : w - 6, mid_y + 4 : h - 6] = 3
            self.obstacle_grid[14:22, 13:17] = 2
            self.obstacle_grid[w - 24 : w - 14, 13:18] = 2
            self.obstacle_grid[16:24, h - 17 : h - 12] = 2

            self.base_pos = np.array([9.0, h - 9.0], dtype=np.float32)
            self.goal_pos = np.array([w - 9.0, 9.0], dtype=np.float32)
            self.floor_grid[3:15, h - 15 : h - 3] = 1
            self.chargers = [np.array([9.0, h - 9.0], dtype=np.float32)]

        elif map_key == "ANTARCTIC":
            self.base_pos = np.array([9.0, h / 2.0], dtype=np.float32)
            self.goal_pos = np.array([w - 9.0, h / 2.0], dtype=np.float32)
            self.floor_grid[2:15, h // 2 - 10 : h // 2 + 10] = 1
            self.floor_grid[w - 15 : w - 2, h // 2 - 10 : h // 2 + 10] = 2

            self.obstacle_grid[24:27, 1 : h // 2 - 6] = 1
            self.obstacle_grid[24:27, h // 2 + 6 : h - 1] = 1

            self.obstacle_grid[44:47, 9 : h - 9] = 1
            self.obstacle_grid[44:47, h // 2 - 4 : h // 2 + 4] = 0

            self.obstacle_grid[64:67, 1 : h // 2 - 8] = 1
            self.obstacle_grid[64:67, h // 2 + 8 : h - 1] = 1

            self.hazard_grid[28:43, h // 2 - 9 : h // 2 + 9] = 1
            self.hazard_grid[48:63, h // 2 - 7 : h // 2 + 7] = 1
            self.hazard_grid[34:56, 3:8] = 1
            self.hazard_grid[34:56, h - 8 : h - 3] = 1
            self.chargers = []

        elif map_key == "INDUSTRIAL":
            for sx in [w // 3, 2 * w // 3]:
                self.obstacle_grid[sx - 1 : sx + 1, 5 : h - 5] = 1
                self.obstacle_grid[sx - 1 : sx + 1, h // 4 - 3 : h // 4 + 3] = 0
                self.obstacle_grid[sx - 1 : sx + 1, 3 * h // 4 - 3 : 3 * h // 4 + 3] = 0

            self.obstacle_grid[10 : w - 10, h // 2 - 1 : h // 2 + 1] = 2
            self.obstacle_grid[w // 6 - 3 : w // 6 + 3, h // 2 - 1 : h // 2 + 1] = 0
            self.obstacle_grid[w // 2 - 4 : w // 2 + 4, h // 2 - 1 : h // 2 + 1] = 0
            self.obstacle_grid[5 * w // 6 - 3 : 5 * w // 6 + 3, h // 2 - 1 : h // 2 + 1] = 0

            self.hazard_grid[w // 3 + 4 : 2 * w // 3 - 4, h // 2 - 6 : h // 2 + 6] = 1
            self.base_pos = np.array([10.0, h / 2.0], dtype=np.float32)
            self.goal_pos = np.array([w - 10.0, h / 2.0], dtype=np.float32)
            self.floor_grid[2:15, h // 2 - 9 : h // 2 + 9] = 1
            self.chargers = [np.array([10.0, h / 2.0], dtype=np.float32)]

        elif map_key == "ARENA":
            cx, cy = w // 2, h // 2
            self.obstacle_grid[cx - 5 : cx + 5, cy - 5 : cy + 5] = 2
            for dx, dy in [(-20, -13), (16, -13), (-20, 9), (16, 9)]:
                self.obstacle_grid[cx + dx : cx + dx + 5, cy + dy : cy + dy + 5] = 1

            self.base_pos = np.array([10.0, cy], dtype=np.float32)
            self.goal_pos = np.array([w - 10.0, cy], dtype=np.float32)
            self.floor_grid[2:14, cy - 11 : cy + 11] = 1
            self.floor_grid[w - 14 : w - 2, cy - 11 : cy + 11] = 2
            self.chargers = [
                np.array([cx, 8.0], dtype=np.float32),
                np.array([cx, h - 8.0], dtype=np.float32),
            ]

        if self.mode == "ANTARCTIC":
            self.chargers = []

        self._precompute_vector_fields()

    def reset(self, agents: List[RobotAgent]) -> None:
        self._build_map(self.map_key)
        self.dirt_grid.fill(0)
        self.combat_events.clear()
        self.energy_share_events.clear()
        self.comm_events.clear()
        self.charge_events.clear()
        self.role_events.clear()
        self._dead_notified.clear()
        self.event_log.clear()
        self.comm_bus.reset()

        self.tick_count = 0
        self.step_kills = 0
        self.total_dirt_spawned = 0
        self.total_dirt_cleaned = 0
        self.total_energy_transfers = 0
        self.total_energy_shared_amount = 0.0
        self.total_messages = 0
        self.last_step_ai_decisions = 0

        initial_dirt = self.initial_dirt
        if self.mode == "ANTARCTIC":
            initial_dirt = max(35, self.initial_dirt // 3)
        elif self.mode == "COOP":
            initial_dirt = max(155, int(self.initial_dirt * 1.3))

        self.spawn_dirt(initial_dirt)

        free_coords = np.argwhere(self.obstacle_grid == 0)
        for i, agent in enumerate(agents):
            agent.energy = agent.MAX_ENERGY
            agent.alive = True
            agent.steps_alive = 0
            agent.dirt_cleaned = 0
            agent.kills = 0
            agent.damage_dealt = 0.0
            agent.distance_traveled = 0.0
            agent.energy_used = 0.0
            agent.energy_given = 0.0
            agent.energy_received = 0.0
            agent.messages_sent = 0
            agent.messages_received = 0
            agent.assists = 0
            agent.ai_decisions_count = 0
            agent.reached_goal = False
            agent.returned_to_base = False
            agent.mission_aborted = False
            agent.death_reason = "AktĂ­v"
            agent.state = "CLEANING"
            agent.trail.clear()
            agent.inbox.clear()
            agent.recent_comm_links.clear()
            agent.memory = type(agent.memory)()
            agent.known_partners.clear()
            agent.reset_multiagent_state()
            agent.assigned_sector = i % 6
            agent.decision_log = [f"[T:0] KĂĽldetĂ©s indult ({self.mode})."]

            if self.mode in ("ANTARCTIC", "COOP"):
                start_zone = np.argwhere((self.obstacle_grid == 0) & (self.floor_grid == 1))
                if len(start_zone) > 0:
                    idx = self.rng.integers(0, len(start_zone))
                    agent.pos = start_zone[idx].astype(np.float32) + 0.5
                else:
                    agent.pos = self.base_pos + self.rng.uniform(-4.0, 4.0, size=2).astype(np.float32)
            elif self.mode == "TDM":
                if agent.team_id == 1:
                    zone = np.argwhere((self.obstacle_grid == 0) & (np.arange(self.width)[:, None] < 16))
                elif agent.team_id == 2:
                    zone = np.argwhere((self.obstacle_grid == 0) & (np.arange(self.width)[:, None] > self.width - 17))
                else:
                    zone = np.argwhere(
                        (self.obstacle_grid == 0)
                        & (np.arange(self.width)[:, None] > self.width // 3)
                        & (np.arange(self.width)[:, None] < 2 * self.width // 3)
                    )
                if len(zone) > 0:
                    idx = self.rng.integers(0, len(zone))
                    agent.pos = zone[idx].astype(np.float32) + 0.5
                else:
                    idx = self.rng.integers(0, len(free_coords))
                    agent.pos = free_coords[idx].astype(np.float32) + 0.5
            else:
                idx = self.rng.integers(0, len(free_coords))
                agent.pos = free_coords[idx].astype(np.float32) + 0.5

            agent.snap_render_state()

        coordination.assign_roles(agents, self.mode, tick=0)       # stabil szerepek a genombĂłl + csapatĂ¶sszetĂ©tel
        counts: Dict[str, int] = {}
        for a in agents:
            counts[a.role_id] = counts.get(a.role_id, 0) + 1
        self.add_log(0, "RENDSZER", "SZEREPEK", "Szerepek: " + ", ".join(
            f"{coordination.ROLE_LABEL[r]} x{n}" for r, n in sorted(counts.items())))
        self.add_log(0, "RENDSZER", "KĂśLDETĂ‰S", f"{self.mode} elindĂ­tva a(z) {MAP_CATALOG[self.map_key]['name']} 3D pĂˇlyĂˇn.")

    def spawn_dirt(self, count: int) -> None:
        if count <= 0:
            return
        free_mask = (self.obstacle_grid == 0) & (self.dirt_grid == 0) & (self.floor_grid != 1)
        free_coords = np.argwhere(free_mask)
        if len(free_coords) == 0:
            return

        actual_count = min(count, len(free_coords))
        chosen_idx = self.rng.choice(len(free_coords), size=actual_count, replace=False)
        chosen = free_coords[chosen_idx]
        intensities = self.rng.choice([1, 2, 3], size=actual_count, p=[0.60, 0.30, 0.10]).astype(np.int8)
        self.dirt_grid[chosen[:, 0], chosen[:, 1]] = intensities
        self.total_dirt_spawned += int(intensities.sum())

    def _get_sector_info(self, sector_id: int) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, int]:
        col = sector_id % 3
        row = sector_id // 3
        sw = self.width // 3
        sh = self.height // 2
        x0, x1 = col * sw, min(self.width, (col + 1) * sw)
        y0, y1 = row * sh, min(self.height, (row + 1) * sh)
        center = np.array([(x0 + x1) / 2.0, (y0 + y1) / 2.0], dtype=np.float32)
        dirt_count = int((self.dirt_grid[x0:x1, y0:y1] > 0).sum())
        return center, dirt_count

    def step(self, agents: List[RobotAgent]) -> Tuple[int, int]:
        self.tick_count += 1
        self.combat_events.clear()
        self.energy_share_events.clear()
        self.comm_events.clear()
        self.charge_events.clear()
        self.last_step_ai_decisions = 0

        for change in coordination.rebalance_roles(agents, self.mode, self.tick_count):   # ritka, indokolt szerepcsere
            self.add_log(self.tick_count, "KOORDINĂTOR", "SZEREP", change)

        living_agents = [a for a in agents if a.alive]
        num_alive = len(living_agents)

        if num_alive == 0:
            return 0, int((self.dirt_grid > 0).sum())

        delivered = self.comm_bus.process_deliveries(self.tick_count, living_agents, self.mode)
        self.comm_events.extend(delivered)

        positions = np.array([a.pos for a in living_agents], dtype=np.float32)
        diff_agents = positions[:, np.newaxis, :] - positions[np.newaxis, :, :]
        dist_agents = np.linalg.norm(diff_agents, axis=-1)
        np.fill_diagonal(dist_agents, np.inf)

        charger_arr = np.array(self.chargers, dtype=np.float32).reshape(-1, 2)
        dirt_coords = np.argwhere(self.dirt_grid > 0).astype(np.float32) + 0.5
        sector_cache = {s: self._get_sector_info(s) for s in range(6)}

        for i, agent in enumerate(living_agents):
            s_range = agent.SENSOR_RANGE
            visible_dirt_count = 0
            dirt_dist = 999.0
            dirt_vec = np.zeros(2, dtype=np.float32)

            if len(dirt_coords) > 0:
                d_vecs = dirt_coords - agent.pos
                d_dists = np.linalg.norm(d_vecs, axis=1)
                vis_mask = d_dists <= s_range
                visible_dirt_count = int(vis_mask.sum())
                if visible_dirt_count > 0:
                    min_d_idx = int(np.argmin(d_dists))
                    dirt_dist = float(d_dists[min_d_idx])
                    dirt_vec = d_vecs[min_d_idx] / max(dirt_dist, 1e-5)

            agent_dist, neighbor_energy = 999.0, 0.0
            agent_vec = np.zeros(2, dtype=np.float32)
            neighbor_id: Optional[int] = None
            neighbor_name: str = "TEAM"

            enemy_dist, enemy_energy = 999.0, 0.0
            enemy_vec = np.zeros(2, dtype=np.float32)

            ally_low_dist = 999.0
            ally_low_vec = np.zeros(2, dtype=np.float32)
            ally_low_name = "TEAM"
            ally_low_id: Optional[int] = None

            if num_alive > 1:
                min_a_idx = int(np.argmin(dist_agents[i]))
                raw_a_dist = float(dist_agents[i, min_a_idx])
                if raw_a_dist <= s_range:
                    agent_dist = raw_a_dist
                    other_nb = living_agents[min_a_idx]
                    agent_vec = (other_nb.pos - agent.pos) / max(agent_dist, 1e-5)
                    neighbor_energy = other_nb.energy
                    neighbor_id = other_nb.id
                    neighbor_name = other_nb.name

                for j, other in enumerate(living_agents):
                    if i == j:
                        continue
                    d_ij = float(dist_agents[i, j])
                    if d_ij > s_range:
                        continue

                    is_enemy = (self.mode != "TDM") or (other.team_id != agent.team_id)
                    if is_enemy and d_ij < enemy_dist:
                        enemy_dist = d_ij
                        enemy_vec = (other.pos - agent.pos) / max(d_ij, 1e-5)
                        enemy_energy = other.energy

                    is_ally = (self.mode in ("COOP", "ANTARCTIC")) or (
                        self.mode == "TDM" and other.team_id == agent.team_id
                    )
                    if is_ally and other.energy_ratio < 0.35 and not other.reached_goal and d_ij < ally_low_dist:
                        ally_low_dist = d_ij
                        ally_low_vec = (other.pos - agent.pos) / max(d_ij, 1e-5)
                        ally_low_name = other.name
                        ally_low_id = other.id

            charger_dist = 999.0
            charger_vec = np.zeros(2, dtype=np.float32)
            if len(self.chargers) > 0:
                c_dists = np.linalg.norm(charger_arr - agent.pos, axis=1)      # vektorizĂˇlt (sok tĂ¶ltĹ‘ nagy pĂˇlyĂˇn)
                min_c = int(np.argmin(c_dists))
                charger_dist = float(c_dists[min_c])
                charger_vec = (charger_arr[min_c] - agent.pos) / max(charger_dist, 1e-5)

            sec_center, sec_dirt = sector_cache[agent.assigned_sector % 6]
            cx = int(np.clip(agent.pos[0], 0, self.width - 1))
            cy = int(np.clip(agent.pos[1], 0, self.height - 1))

            sensor_data: Dict[str, Any] = {
                "dirt_vec": dirt_vec,
                "dirt_dist": dirt_dist,
                "visible_dirt_count": visible_dirt_count,
                "agent_vec": agent_vec,
                "agent_dist": agent_dist,
                "agent_energy": neighbor_energy,
                "neighbor_id": neighbor_id,
                "neighbor_name": neighbor_name,
                "enemy_vec": enemy_vec,
                "enemy_dist": enemy_dist,
                "enemy_energy": enemy_energy,
                "ally_low_vec": ally_low_vec,
                "ally_low_dist": ally_low_dist,
                "ally_low_name": ally_low_name,
                "ally_low_id": ally_low_id,
                "charger_vec": charger_vec,
                "charger_dist": charger_dist,
                "base_pos": self.base_pos,
                "goal_pos": self.goal_pos,
                "sector_center": sec_center,
                "sector_dirt_count": sec_dirt,
                "wall_vec": self.wall_field[cx, cy],
                "hazard_vec": self.hazard_field[cx, cy],
            }

            was_alive = agent.alive
            was_at_goal = agent.reached_goal
            outgoing_specs = agent.decide_and_move(
                sensor_data,
                self.width,
                self.height,
                self.obstacle_grid,
                mode=self.mode,
                tick=self.tick_count,
                hazard_grid=self.hazard_grid,
            )
            self.last_step_ai_decisions += 1

            if was_alive and not agent.alive:
                self.add_log(self.tick_count, agent.name, "ENERGIA", f"Kiesett ({agent.death_reason}).")
            if not was_at_goal and agent.reached_goal:
                self.add_log(self.tick_count, agent.name, "KĂśLDETĂ‰S", "ElĂ©rte a B kutatĂłĂˇllomĂˇst!")

            if self.comm_bus.enabled:
                for spec in outgoing_specs:
                    if self.comm_bus.enqueue_from_spec(agent, spec, self.tick_count, self.rng) is not None:
                        self.total_messages += 1

        for agent in living_agents:
            if not agent.alive:
                continue
            cx, cy = int(agent.pos[0]), int(agent.pos[1])
            x0, x1 = max(1, cx - 1), min(self.width - 1, cx + 2)
            y0, y1 = max(1, cy - 1), min(self.height - 1, cy + 2)

            patch = self.dirt_grid[x0:x1, y0:y1]
            cleaned_amount = int(patch.sum())
            if cleaned_amount > 0:
                self.dirt_grid[x0:x1, y0:y1] = 0
                agent.dirt_cleaned += cleaned_amount
                self.total_dirt_cleaned += cleaned_amount
                energy_gain = cleaned_amount * (11.0 if self.mode != "COOP" else 8.5)
                agent.energy = min(agent.MAX_ENERGY, agent.energy + energy_gain)
                if cleaned_amount >= 4:
                    self.add_log(
                        self.tick_count,
                        agent.name,
                        "TAKARĂŤTĂS",
                        f"SĹ±rĹ± szennyezĹ‘dĂ©si gĂłcot tisztĂ­tott meg (+{cleaned_amount} egysĂ©g).",
                    )

            if len(charger_arr) > 0 and agent.energy < agent.MAX_ENERGY:
                near = np.linalg.norm(charger_arr - agent.pos, axis=1) < 2.8
                for _ in range(int(near.sum())):          # minden kĂ¶zeli tĂ¶ltĹ‘ kĂĽlĂ¶n tĂ¶lt (eredeti viselkedĂ©s)
                    agent.energy = min(agent.MAX_ENERGY, agent.energy + 1.8)
                    if agent.id not in self.charge_events:
                        self.charge_events.append(agent.id)

        self._resolve_energy_sharing(living_agents)

        if self.mode in ("CLASSIC", "TDM"):
            self._resolve_combat(living_agents)

        # kiesett robotok: minden Ă©lĹ‘ tĂˇrs tĂ¶rli a rĂˇjuk mutatĂł hivatkozĂˇsokat (nincs Ă©rvĂ©nytelen cĂ­mzett / foglalĂˇs)
        for a in agents:
            if not a.alive and a.id not in self._dead_notified:
                self._dead_notified.add(a.id)
                for b in agents:
                    if b.alive:
                        b.forget_peer(a.id, self.tick_count)

        if self.mode == "CLASSIC":
            self.spawn_dirt(self.dirt_spawn_rate)
        elif self.mode == "TDM" and self.tick_count % 2 == 0:
            self.spawn_dirt(max(1, self.dirt_spawn_rate // 2))
        elif self.mode == "COOP" and self.tick_count % 8 == 0 and self.dirt_spawn_rate > 0:
            self.spawn_dirt(1)

        still_alive = sum(1 for a in agents if a.alive)
        return still_alive, int(self.dirt_grid.sum())

    def _resolve_energy_sharing(self, living_agents: List[RobotAgent]) -> None:
        n = len(living_agents)
        if n < 2:
            return

        share_range = 3.4
        for i in range(n):
            donor = living_agents[i]
            if not donor.alive or donor.energy_ratio < 0.55 or donor.genome.cooperation < 0.38:
                continue

            for j in range(n):
                if i == j:
                    continue
                receiver = living_agents[j]
                if not receiver.alive or receiver.energy_ratio > 0.38 or receiver.reached_goal:
                    continue
                if self.mode == "TDM" and donor.team_id != receiver.team_id:
                    continue

                dist = float(np.linalg.norm(donor.pos - receiver.pos))
                if dist <= share_range:
                    transfer = min(13.0, donor.energy - 40.0, receiver.MAX_ENERGY - receiver.energy)
                    if transfer > 2.0:
                        donor.energy -= transfer
                        received = transfer * 0.85
                        receiver.energy = min(receiver.MAX_ENERGY, receiver.energy + received)
                        donor.energy_given += transfer
                        receiver.energy_received += received
                        donor.assists += 1
                        self.total_energy_transfers += 1
                        self.total_energy_shared_amount += received
                        self.energy_share_events.append((donor.pos.copy(), receiver.pos.copy()))
                        self.add_log(
                            self.tick_count,
                            donor.name,
                            "ENERGIA",
                            f"Ătadott {received:.1f} energiĂˇt {receiver.name} rĂ©szĂ©re.",
                        )
                        break

    def _resolve_combat(self, living_agents: List[RobotAgent]) -> None:
        n = len(living_agents)
        if n < 2:
            return

        positions = np.array([a.pos for a in living_agents], dtype=np.float32)
        diff = positions[:, np.newaxis, :] - positions[np.newaxis, :, :]
        dists = np.linalg.norm(diff, axis=-1)

        attack_range = 2.4
        for i in range(n):
            attacker = living_agents[i]
            if not attacker.alive or attacker.state != "HUNTING":
                continue

            targets_idx = np.where((dists[i] < attack_range) & (dists[i] > 0.0))[0]
            for j in targets_idx:
                victim = living_agents[j]
                if not victim.alive:
                    continue
                if self.mode == "TDM" and attacker.team_id == victim.team_id:
                    continue

                drain_amount = min(victim.energy, 13.0 + 16.0 * attacker.genome.aggressiveness)
                victim.energy -= drain_amount
                net_gain = max(0.0, drain_amount * 0.72 - 2.0)
                attacker.energy = min(attacker.MAX_ENERGY, attacker.energy + net_gain)
                attacker.damage_dealt += drain_amount

                self.combat_events.append((attacker.pos.copy(), victim.pos.copy()))
                victim.last_attacker_id = attacker.id            # a megtĂˇmadott tudja, ki tĂˇmadta (veszĂ©lyjelzĂ©shez)
                victim.last_attacked_tick = self.tick_count
                victim.last_attacker_pos = attacker.pos.copy()

                if victim.energy <= 0.0:
                    victim.energy = 0.0
                    victim.alive = False
                    victim.state = "DISABLED"
                    victim.death_reason = f"Harci treff ({attacker.name})"
                    attacker.kills += 1
                    self.step_kills += 1
                    self.add_log(
                        self.tick_count,
                        attacker.name,
                        "HARC",
                        f"LemerĂ­tette Ă©s kiĂĽtĂ¶tte {victim.name} egysĂ©get!",
                    )
                break
=== FILE: C:\Users\dave\Desktop\egyetemgeci\5. felev\Projektmunka\project geminivel\3d verzio\archive\3d\evolution.py ===
import csv
import os
from datetime import datetime
import numpy as np
from typing import List, Dict, Optional
from agent import RobotAgent, Genome


class EvolutionaryAlgorithm:
    """
    PopulĂˇciĂł-menedzsment, kĂĽldetĂ©sspecifikus fitnesz kiĂ©rtĂ©kelĂ©s, szelekciĂł Ă©s CSV exportĂˇlĂˇs.
    Minden jĂˇtĂ©kmĂłd kĂĽlĂ¶n statisztikai idĹ‘sort kap.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        pop_size: int = 20,
        mutation_rate: float = 0.15,
        swap_rate: float = 0.06,
        elite_ratio: float = 0.16,
        survival_weight: float = 1.0,
        dirt_weight: float = 25.0,
        mode: str = "CLASSIC",
        num_teams: int = 2,
        start_energy: float = 100.0,
    ):
        self.pop_size: int = pop_size
        self.mutation_rate: float = mutation_rate
        self.swap_rate: float = swap_rate
        self.elite_ratio: float = elite_ratio
        self.survival_weight: float = survival_weight
        self.dirt_weight: float = dirt_weight
        self.mode: str = mode
        self.num_teams: int = num_teams
        self.start_energy: float = start_energy

        self.generation: int = 1
        self.agents: List[RobotAgent] = self._create_initial_agents()

        self.history: Dict[str, List[float]] = self._empty_history()
        self.histories_by_mode: Dict[str, Dict[str, List[float]]] = {
            "CLASSIC": self._empty_history(),
            "ANTARCTIC": self._empty_history(),
            "COOP": self._empty_history(),
            "TDM": self._empty_history(),
        }

    def _empty_history(self) -> Dict[str, List[float]]:
        return {
            "generation": [],
            "max_fitness": [],
            "avg_fitness": [],
            "survival_rate": [],
            "return_or_goal_rate": [],
            "avg_aggressiveness": [],
            "avg_risk_tolerance": [],
            "avg_cooperation": [],
            "avg_speed": [],
            "total_kills": [],
            "total_dirt": [],
            "total_energy_shared": [],
            "total_messages": [],
        }

    def _create_initial_agents(self, genomes: Optional[List[Genome]] = None) -> List[RobotAgent]:
        agents: List[RobotAgent] = []
        team_prefixes = {0: "R", 1: "ALP", 2: "BRV", 3: "GAM"}
        for i in range(self.pop_size):
            team_id = (i % self.num_teams) + 1 if self.mode == "TDM" else 0
            prefix = team_prefixes.get(team_id, "R")
            genome = genomes[i] if (genomes and i < len(genomes)) else Genome.random()
            agents.append(
                RobotAgent(
                    agent_id=i,
                    pos=np.zeros(2, dtype=np.float32),
                    genome=genome,
                    team_id=team_id,
                    name=f"{prefix}-{i + 1:02d}",
                    max_energy=self.start_energy,
                )
            )
        return agents

    def compute_fitness(
        self, agent: RobotAgent, goal_pos: Optional[np.ndarray] = None
    ) -> float:
        active_factor = min(1.0, agent.distance_traveled / 25.0)

        if self.mode == "ANTARCTIC":
            goal_bonus = 450.0 if agent.reached_goal else 0.0
            remaining_energy_bonus = agent.energy * 2.2 if agent.reached_goal else 0.0
            dist_penalty = 0.0
            if goal_pos is not None and not agent.reached_goal:
                dist_to_goal = float(np.linalg.norm(goal_pos - agent.pos))
                dist_penalty = max(0.0, 220.0 - dist_to_goal * 2.4)
            coop_bonus = agent.energy_given * 3.5 + agent.assists * 25.0
            survival_bonus = (60.0 if agent.alive else 0.0) * active_factor
            return float(goal_bonus + remaining_energy_bonus + dist_penalty + coop_bonus + survival_bonus)

        elif self.mode == "COOP":
            clean_score = agent.dirt_cleaned * 30.0
            base_bonus = 240.0 if (agent.alive and agent.returned_to_base and agent.dirt_cleaned > 0) else 0.0
            alive_bonus = 55.0 if agent.alive else 0.0
            coop_score = agent.energy_given * 3.2 + min(agent.messages_received + agent.messages_sent, 16) * 4.5
            return float(clean_score + base_bonus + alive_bonus + coop_score)

        elif self.mode == "TDM":
            combat_score = agent.damage_dealt * 1.6 + agent.kills * 95.0
            team_support = agent.energy_given * 2.8 + agent.dirt_cleaned * 12.0
            survival_score = agent.steps_alive * 0.7 * active_factor + (80.0 if agent.alive else 0.0)
            return float(combat_score + team_support + survival_score)

        else:
            return float(
                agent.steps_alive * self.survival_weight * max(0.3, active_factor)
                + agent.dirt_cleaned * self.dirt_weight
            )

    def _tournament_selection(
        self, agents: List[RobotAgent], fitnesses: np.ndarray, k: int = 3
    ) -> Genome:
        indices = np.random.choice(len(agents), size=min(k, len(agents)), replace=False)
        best_idx = indices[np.argmax(fitnesses[indices])]
        return agents[best_idx].genome

    def evolve_next_generation(
        self, goal_pos: Optional[np.ndarray] = None
    ) -> List[RobotAgent]:
        fitnesses = np.array(
            [self.compute_fitness(a, goal_pos=goal_pos) for a in self.agents],
            dtype=np.float32,
        )

        n = max(1, len(self.agents))
        surv_rate = sum(1 for a in self.agents if a.alive) / n * 100.0
        if self.mode == "ANTARCTIC":
            goal_rate = sum(1 for a in self.agents if a.reached_goal) / n * 100.0
        elif self.mode == "COOP":
            goal_rate = sum(1 for a in self.agents if a.alive and a.returned_to_base) / n * 100.0
        else:
            goal_rate = surv_rate

        record = {
            "generation": self.generation,
            "max_fitness": float(np.max(fitnesses)),
            "avg_fitness": float(np.mean(fitnesses)),
            "survival_rate": float(surv_rate),
            "return_or_goal_rate": float(goal_rate),
            "avg_aggressiveness": float(np.mean([a.genome.aggressiveness for a in self.agents])),
            "avg_risk_tolerance": float(np.mean([a.genome.risk_tolerance for a in self.agents])),
            "avg_cooperation": float(np.mean([a.genome.cooperation for a in self.agents])),
            "avg_speed": float(np.mean([a.genome.speed for a in self.agents])),
            "total_kills": int(sum(a.kills for a in self.agents)),
            "total_dirt": int(sum(a.dirt_cleaned for a in self.agents)),
            "total_energy_shared": float(sum(a.energy_given for a in self.agents)),
            "total_messages": int(sum(a.messages_sent for a in self.agents)),
        }

        for key, val in record.items():
            self.history[key].append(val)
            self.histories_by_mode[self.mode][key].append(val)

        sorted_indices = np.argsort(fitnesses)[::-1]
        num_elites = max(2, int(self.pop_size * self.elite_ratio))

        new_genomes: List[Genome] = []
        for i in range(num_elites):
            new_genomes.append(Genome(genes=self.agents[sorted_indices[i]].genome.genes.copy()))

        while len(new_genomes) < self.pop_size:
            parent1 = self._tournament_selection(self.agents, fitnesses, k=3)
            parent2 = self._tournament_selection(self.agents, fitnesses, k=3)

            child_genome = Genome.crossover(parent1, parent2)
            child_genome.mutate_shift(mutation_rate=self.mutation_rate, sigma=0.12)
            child_genome.mutate_swap(swap_rate=self.swap_rate)
            new_genomes.append(child_genome)

        self.agents = self._create_initial_agents(genomes=new_genomes)
        self.generation += 1
        return self.agents

    def export_history_to_csv(self, filename: Optional[str] = None) -> str:
        if len(self.history["generation"]) == 0:
            return "Nincs menthetĹ‘ generĂˇciĂłs adat (futtass le legalĂˇbb 1 generĂˇciĂłt)!"

        if filename is None:
            stamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
            filename = f"stats_{self.mode.lower()}_{stamp}.csv"

        keys = list(self.history.keys())
        rows = zip(*(self.history[k] for k in keys))

        filepath = os.path.abspath(filename)
        with open(filepath, mode="w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            writer = csv.writer(f)
            writer.writerow(keys)
            writer.writerows(rows)

        return f"Statisztika CSV mentve: {os.path.basename(filepath)}"
=== FILE: C:\Users\dave\Desktop\egyetemgeci\5. felev\Projektmunka\project geminivel\3d verzio\archive\3d\gui_manager.py ===
"""
KĂ¶zponti GUI-kezelĹ‘ a 3D robotporszĂ­vĂł-szimulĂˇciĂłhoz.

Feladatai:
  * RESPONSIVE ELRENDEZĂ‰S: minden panel egy "horgony" (sarok / Ă©l) kĂ¶rĂ© van rendezve.
    A panelek tartalma az eredeti, 16:9-es referencia-koordinĂˇtĂˇkkal kĂ©szĂĽlt; a kezelĹ‘
    a panel gyĂ¶kĂ©r-entitĂˇsĂˇt Ăşgy pozicionĂˇlja Ă©s skĂˇlĂˇzza, hogy a referencia-sarok
    mindig a tĂ©nyleges ablak megfelelĹ‘ sarkĂˇra essen. ĂŤgy a gyerekelemek koordinĂˇtĂˇit
    nem kell ĂˇtĂ­rni, Ă©s az ablakmĂ©ret/kĂ©parĂˇny vĂˇltozĂˇsakor csak a nĂ©hĂˇny gyĂ¶kĂ©r-entitĂˇs
    pozĂ­ciĂłja frissĂĽl (nincs GUI-ĂşjraĂ©pĂ­tĂ©s).
  * LĂTHATĂ“SĂG hĂˇrom, egymĂˇstĂłl fĂĽggetlen szinten:
        1. globĂˇlis GUI-lĂˇthatĂłsĂˇg (Tab)           -> gui.global_visible
        2. panelenkĂ©nti helyi lĂˇthatĂłsĂˇg (beĂˇllĂ­tĂˇs) -> panel.local_visible
        3. csoportos Ă¶sszecsukĂˇs (oldalsĂł fĂĽl)      -> gui.group_collapsed[csoport]
    Egy panel akkor lĂˇtszik, ha mindhĂˇrom megengedi. A hĂˇrom Ăˇllapot kĂĽlĂ¶n tĂˇrolĂłdik,
    ezĂ©rt a globĂˇlis visszakapcsolĂˇs pontosan a korĂˇbbi egyĂ©ni Ăˇllapotot ĂˇllĂ­tja vissza.

A modul nem importĂˇl UrsinĂˇt: a panelek gyĂ¶kere bĂˇrmilyen, `enabled`, `x`, `y`, `scale`
attribĂştumokkal rendelkezĹ‘ objektum lehet (Ursina Entity, vagy tesztben egy egyszerĹ±
helyettesĂ­tĹ‘ objektum).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Callable, Dict, Iterable, List, Optional, Tuple

REF_ASPECT: float = 16.0 / 9.0          # az eredeti HUD ezen a kĂ©parĂˇnyon kĂ©szĂĽlt
REF_HALF_W: float = REF_ASPECT / 2.0    # a referencia-kĂ©pernyĹ‘ fĂ©l szĂ©lessĂ©ge (ui-egysĂ©g)
REF_HALF_H: float = 0.5                 # az Ursina camera.ui fĂĽggĹ‘leges fĂ©l kiterjedĂ©se
MIN_SCALE: float = 0.5                  # ennĂ©l jobban nem zsugorĂ­tjuk a paneleket

# horgony -> (vĂ­zszintes, fĂĽggĹ‘leges) irĂˇny: -1 = bal/alsĂł, 0 = kĂ¶zĂ©p, +1 = jobb/felsĹ‘
ANCHORS: Dict[str, Tuple[int, int]] = {
    "TL": (-1, 1), "TC": (0, 1), "TR": (1, 1),
    "CL": (-1, 0), "C": (0, 0), "CR": (1, 0),
    "BL": (-1, -1), "BC": (0, -1), "BR": (1, -1),
}

Rect = Tuple[float, float, float, float]  # (kĂ¶zĂ©ppont x, kĂ¶zĂ©ppont y, szĂ©lessĂ©g, magassĂˇg) referencia-koordinĂˇtĂˇban


class GuiPanel:
    """Egy Ă¶nĂˇllĂłan ki/be kapcsolhatĂł, horgonyzott panel."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        name: str,
        root,
        anchor: str,
        title: str = "",
        group: str = "",
        hideable: bool = True,
        default_visible: bool = True,
        rects: Iterable[Rect] = (),
        depends_any: Iterable[str] = (),
        on_layout: Optional[Callable[["GuiPanel", float, float], None]] = None,
    ):
        if anchor not in ANCHORS:
            raise ValueError(f"Ismeretlen horgony: {anchor}")
        self.name = name
        self.root = root
        self.anchor = anchor
        self.title = title or name
        self.group = group
        self.hideable = hideable            # False: a globĂˇlis elrejtĂ©s nem Ă©rinti (pl. fordulĂłĂ¶sszegzĹ‘)
        self.local_visible = default_visible
        self.rects: List[Rect] = list(rects)
        self.depends_any: Tuple[str, ...] = tuple(depends_any)
        self.on_layout = on_layout
        self.effective: Optional[bool] = None   # utoljĂˇra az entitĂˇsra alkalmazott Ăˇllapot
        self.px: float = 0.0                    # a gyĂ¶kĂ©r jelenlegi pozĂ­ciĂłja
        self.py: float = 0.0
        self.scale: float = 1.0


class GuiManager:
    def __init__(self, on_change: Optional[Callable[[], None]] = None):
        self.panels: Dict[str, GuiPanel] = {}
        self._order: List[str] = []
        self.global_visible: bool = True
        self.group_collapsed: Dict[str, bool] = {}
        self.aspect: float = REF_ASPECT
        self.scale: float = 1.0
        self.on_change = on_change

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ regisztrĂˇciĂł
    def add_panel(self, name: str, root, anchor: str, **kwargs) -> GuiPanel:
        if name in self.panels:
            raise ValueError(f"MĂˇr lĂ©tezĹ‘ panel: {name}")
        panel = GuiPanel(name, root, anchor, **kwargs)
        self.panels[name] = panel
        self._order.append(name)
        if panel.group and panel.group not in self.group_collapsed:
            self.group_collapsed[panel.group] = False
        return panel

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ elrendezĂ©s
    @staticmethod
    def scale_for_aspect(aspect: float) -> float:
        """Keskenyebb (nem 16:9-es) ablakban egyenletesen zsugorĂ­tja a paneleket."""
        if aspect <= 0:
            return 1.0
        return max(MIN_SCALE, min(1.0, aspect / REF_ASPECT))

    def layout(self, aspect: float) -> None:
        """ĂšjraszĂˇmolja minden panel gyĂ¶kerĂ©nek pozĂ­ciĂłjĂˇt/mĂ©retĂ©t. Csak mĂ©retvĂˇltozĂˇskor hĂ­vandĂł."""
        if aspect <= 0:
            return
        self.aspect = float(aspect)
        s = self.scale_for_aspect(self.aspect)
        self.scale = s
        for name in self._order:
            p = self.panels[name]
            kx, ky = ANCHORS[p.anchor]
            ref_x, ref_y = kx * REF_HALF_W, ky * REF_HALF_H          # a sarok a referenciĂˇban
            scr_x, scr_y = kx * self.aspect / 2.0, ky * REF_HALF_H   # ugyanaz a sarok a tĂ©nyleges ablakban
            p.scale = s
            p.px = scr_x - s * ref_x
            p.py = scr_y - s * ref_y
            p.root.x = p.px
            p.root.y = p.py
            p.root.scale = (s, s, 1)
            if p.on_layout is not None:
                p.on_layout(p, self.aspect, s)

    def to_screen(self, panel: GuiPanel, x: float, y: float) -> Tuple[float, float]:
        """Referencia-koordinĂˇtĂˇt a panel aktuĂˇlis tĂ©nyleges kĂ©pernyĹ‘-koordinĂˇtĂˇjĂˇra alakĂ­t."""
        return panel.px + panel.scale * x, panel.py + panel.scale * y

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ lĂˇthatĂłsĂˇg
    def _compute(self, p: GuiPanel, cache: Dict[str, bool]) -> bool:
        if p.name in cache:
            return cache[p.name]
        cache[p.name] = False  # ciklusvĂ©delem
        if p.hideable and not self.global_visible:
            vis = False
        elif p.group and self.group_collapsed.get(p.group, False):
            vis = False
        elif p.depends_any:
            vis = any(self._compute(self.panels[d], cache) for d in p.depends_any if d in self.panels)
        else:
            vis = p.local_visible
        cache[p.name] = vis
        return vis

    def refresh(self) -> None:
        """A meglĂ©vĹ‘ entitĂˇsok enabled ĂˇllapotĂˇt illeszti a hĂˇrom lĂˇthatĂłsĂˇgi szinthez (nincs ĂşjraĂ©pĂ­tĂ©s)."""
        cache: Dict[str, bool] = {}
        for name in self._order:
            p = self.panels[name]
            vis = self._compute(p, cache)
            if vis != p.effective:
                p.effective = vis
                p.root.enabled = vis
        if self.on_change is not None:
            self.on_change()

    def is_visible(self, name: str) -> bool:
        p = self.panels[name]
        if p.effective is None:
            self.refresh()
        return bool(p.effective)

    # globĂˇlis
    def set_global(self, visible: bool) -> None:
        self.global_visible = bool(visible)
        self.refresh()

    def toggle_global(self) -> bool:
        self.set_global(not self.global_visible)
        return self.global_visible

    # helyi
    def set_local(self, name: str, visible: bool) -> None:
        self.panels[name].local_visible = bool(visible)
        self.refresh()

    def toggle_local(self, name: str) -> bool:
        self.set_local(name, not self.panels[name].local_visible)
        return self.panels[name].local_visible

    def is_local(self, name: str) -> bool:
        return self.panels[name].local_visible

    def reset_locals(self, names: Optional[Iterable[str]] = None, value: bool = True) -> None:
        for n in (names if names is not None else self._order):
            p = self.panels[n]
            if not p.depends_any:
                p.local_visible = value
        self.refresh()

    # csoportos Ă¶sszecsukĂˇs
    def set_group_collapsed(self, group: str, collapsed: bool) -> None:
        self.group_collapsed[group] = bool(collapsed)
        self.refresh()

    def toggle_group(self, group: str) -> bool:
        self.set_group_collapsed(group, not self.group_collapsed.get(group, False))
        return self.group_collapsed[group]

    def is_group_collapsed(self, group: str) -> bool:
        return self.group_collapsed.get(group, False)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ egĂ©r / talĂˇlat
    def panel_at(self, mx: float, my: float) -> Optional[str]:
        """Annak a lĂˇthatĂł panelnek a neve, amelynek hĂˇtterĂ©n az (mx, my) ui-pozĂ­ciĂł van."""
        for name in reversed(self._order):
            p = self.panels[name]
            if not self.is_visible(name):
                continue
            for (cx, cy, w, h) in p.rects:
                sx, sy = self.to_screen(p, cx, cy)
                sw, sh = w * p.scale, h * p.scale
                if abs(mx - sx) <= sw / 2.0 and abs(my - sy) <= sh / 2.0:
                    return name
        return None

    def mouse_over_gui(self, mx: float, my: float) -> bool:
        return self.panel_at(mx, my) is not None

def rgb(r: int, g: int, b: int):
    """
    Ursina-szĂ­n 0..255 RGB Ă©rtĂ©kekbĹ‘l.

    FONTOS (a fehĂ©r kĂ©pernyĹ‘ gyĂ¶kĂ©roka): az Ursina color.rgb()/color.rgba()
    0..1 kĂ¶zĂ¶tti lebegĹ‘pontos Ă©rtĂ©ket vĂˇr. Ha 0..255-Ă¶s egĂ©szeket kap, minden
    csatorna 1.0 fĂ¶lĂ© megy Ă©s fehĂ©rre telĂ­tĹ‘dik (hĂˇttĂ©r, gombok, szĂ¶vegek).
    EzĂ©rt a Color-t kĂ¶zvetlenĂĽl, 0..1-re normalizĂˇlva Ă©pĂ­tjĂĽk fel.
    """
    return color.Color(r / 255.0, g / 255.0, b / 255.0, 1.0)


def rgba(r: int, g: int, b: int, a: int = 255):
    """Ursina-szĂ­n 0..255 RGBA Ă©rtĂ©kekbĹ‘l (lĂˇsd rgb())."""
    return color.Color(r / 255.0, g / 255.0, b / 255.0, a / 255.0)


# KĂ¶zpontilag mĂłdosĂ­thatĂł billentyĹ±kiosztĂˇs (a tĂ¶bbi billentyĹ± a handle_input()-ban van)
KEYS: Dict[str, str] = {

CHAR_W_UI = 0.0171   # egy karakter szĂ©lessĂ©ge ui-egysĂ©gben 1.0-s szĂ¶vegskĂˇlĂˇn (a menĂĽ-kĂ©pernyĹ‘kĂ©prĹ‘l kalibrĂˇlva)


def fit_text_scale(text: str, base_scale: float, width_ui: float, min_factor: float = 0.55) -> float:
    """
    A gomb feliratĂˇnak skĂˇlĂˇja Ăşgy, hogy belefĂ©rjen a gomb szĂ©lessĂ©gĂ©be (10% margĂłval), de ne vĂˇljon
    olvashatatlanul kicsivĂ© (min. a kiindulĂł mĂ©ret 55%-a). A panel-skĂˇlĂˇzĂˇs a gombot Ă©s a szĂ¶veget egyarĂˇnt
    arĂˇnyosan kezeli, ezĂ©rt a gomb sajĂˇt (panel-lokĂˇlis) szĂ©lessĂ©ge a mĂ©rvadĂł.
    """
    need = max(1, len(text)) * CHAR_W_UI * base_scale
    avail = width_ui * 0.90
    if need <= avail:
        return base_scale
    return max(base_scale * min_factor, base_scale * avail / need)
